**[00:22:13.820] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000167)  
Honorable Chairman, distinguished members of the Senate Committee on Finance, colleagues, ladies and gentlemen, good morning.

**[00:22:20.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000168)  
Today we seek your support for the Department of Agriculture's proposed 2027 Bill.

**[00:22:25.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000169)  
At the time when our farmers and fisherfolk face a perfect storm, a looming super El Nino, worsening climate risks, and rising costs of fuel fertilizers and vital inputs, pressures compounded by geopolitical shocks beyond our control.

**[00:22:42.460] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000170)  
The Department of Agriculture cannot control the weather or the price of oil halfway around the world, which also affects the price of fertilizers.

**[00:22:51.020] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000171)  
But we can decide on how prepared our farmers are.

**[00:22:55.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000172)  
And we must. We cannot demand cheap food while leaving farmers struggling to afford to produce it.

**[00:23:04.460] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000173)  
Nor can we expect our fisherfolks to keep feeding the nation if doing so means their families remain hungry for a better life.

**[00:23:13.840] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000174)  
Affordable food begins with viable farms. Food security begins with secure livelihoods.

**[00:23:20.200] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000175)  
Our proposed budget is designed to turn these principles into action.

**[00:23:25.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000176)  
We will continue raising productivity through quality seeds, better inputs and modern technologies, expand farm-to-market roads, cold chain systems, and post-harvest facilities,

**[00:23:37.320] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000177)  
and strengthen links between producers and markets so farmers and fisherfolks earn more from what they produce.

**[00:23:44.500] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000178)  
We will also invest in climate resilience, disaster preparedness, and biosecurity.

**[00:23:49.900] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000179)  
Because every harvest lost to drought, every fish catch spoiled in transit,

**[00:23:55.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000180)  
every crop destroyed by disease is not merely an agricultural setback, it is food lost, income lost, and opportunity lost.

**[00:24:03.200] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000181)  
Every peso invested wisely in agriculture can help deliver more than food.

**[00:24:08.200] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000182)  
It can mean more stable prices, stronger rural economies, better paying livelihoods, and greater dignity for millions of Filipinos.

**[00:24:17.200] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000183)  
But public investment must come with public accountability.

**[00:24:21.200] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000184)  
Government assistance must reach small farmers, fisherfolks, and agrarian communities.

**[00:24:25.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000185)  
For whom it is intended.

**[00:24:28.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000186)  
Every program must be measured not simply by how much it spends, but by what it delivers.

**[00:24:33.500] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000187)  
Higher productivity, higher net incomes, fewer post-harvest losses, and food that remains safe and affordable.

**[00:24:40.860] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000188)  
In a moment, Undersecretary Aziz Perez will present the details of our proposed budget and investments that will help us meet these goals.

**[00:24:48.200] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000189)  
But beyond the figures lies a fundamental choice.

**[00:24:51.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000190)  
Whether we will continue asking our food producers to do more,

**[00:24:55.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000191)  
or give them the tools, infrastructure, and protection they need to thrive.

**[00:24:59.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000192)  
Honorable Senators,

**[00:25:01.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000193)  
A nation cannot eat budget figures, but with the right investments,

**[00:25:05.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000194)  
those figures can help put food on every table and a better future within every farmer's reach.

**[00:25:11.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000195)  
On behalf of the Department of Agriculture, I respectfully seek the Senate's support for our proposed 2027 budget.

**[00:25:18.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000196)  
Together, we are committed to a culture that not only feeds the country,

**[00:25:23.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000197)  
but also provides a more sustainable future.

**[00:25:25.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000198)  
A future that will be more productive and a better life for the people who feed us.

**[00:25:29.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000199)  
Thank you very much.

**[00:25:31.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000200)  
Thank you Mr. Chairman and good morning to everybody.

**[00:25:33.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000201)  
Thank you very much, Secretary Kiko Laurel.

**[00:25:37.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000202)  
Before we get on with the presentation by Usec Aziz Perez,

**[00:25:43.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000203)  
we place on record and acknowledge the presence of our former Senate President, currently Senate President Pro Temp, Senator Tito Soto.

**[00:25:53.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000204)  
Thank you, sir.

**[00:25:55.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000205)  
Go ahead, Usec Aziz, with your presentation of the Department's 2027 proposed budget.

**[00:26:05.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000206)  
Go ahead.

**[00:26:07.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000207)  
Good morning, Mr. Chair and to the honorable members of this committee.

**[00:26:11.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000208)  
It is an honor to present the Department's Agriculture FYI, FY2027 National Expenditure Program.

**[00:26:21.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000209)  
Before that, I would like to provide the outline of this presentation.

**[00:26:24.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000210)  
Before you proceed, we'd like to acknowledge the presence of our Senate President,

**[00:26:30.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000211)  
Senator Win Gatchalian and Senate President Gatchalian.

**[00:26:33.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000212)  
Good morning.

**[00:26:34.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000213)  
Go ahead.

**[00:26:35.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000214)  
In this outline, before we dive into the details of the National Expenditure Program,

**[00:26:43.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000215)  
we would like to provide a brief snapshot of the current situation in agriculture and fisheries sector.

**[00:26:50.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000216)  
This will provide a broader context of our programs and investments under the NEP.

**[00:26:56.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000217)  
After this overview, we will walk through the details of the NEP.

**[00:27:02.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000218)  
Of the 2.3 trillion GDP in 2025, agriculture and fisheries sector generated around 1.83 trillion, contributing around 7.9% to the overall economy.

**[00:27:20.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000219)  
The agriculture and fisheries share has steadily declined from 10.1% in 2020 despite remarkable resilience during the pandemic.

**[00:27:32.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000220)  
This highlights the need to accelerate growth and strengthen the sector's contribution to the economy.

**[00:27:39.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000221)  
In terms of stakeholders, we have 10.44 million registered individuals in our SPSA.

**[00:27:48.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000222)  
This is broadly consistent with the PSA estimate of 10 million people engaged in agriculture and fisheries,

**[00:27:56.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000223)  
or an equivalence of about one for every five workers.

**[00:28:01.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000224)  
Our registry will provide a clearer picture as shown in the table.

**[00:28:07.500] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000225)  
Now, poverty among the farmers and fisher folks has declined substantially over the past two decades.

**[00:28:19.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000228)  
Yet, it remains significantly higher than the national average.

**[00:28:24.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000229)  
In 2023, poverty incidence stood at 27% for our farmers and 27.4% for our fisher folks, compared with 15.5% nationwide.

**[00:28:35.740] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000230)  
This indicates that while we have made progress,

**[00:28:40.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000231)  
this has yet to be fully translated into improved livelihoods on those sectors who depend on agriculture.

**[00:28:50.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000234)  
Now, looking deeper into this figure,

**[00:28:54.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000235)  
the 7.9% reflects only the value of production at the farm gate,

**[00:29:01.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000236)  
but not the much greater value generated when agricultural products are processed, transported, and sold to the market.

**[00:29:10.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000237)  
Consider this.

**[00:29:12.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000238)  
There will be no 55 kilogram of rice in Metro Manila without the palay,

**[00:29:16.310] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000240)  
brought at 19 pesos from the farmers.

**[00:29:20.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000241)  
There will be no 366 peso per kilogram liempo if there is no pork produced at 170 pesos per kilogram.

**[00:29:31.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000242)  
There will be no 206 per kilogram chicken without broiler.

**[00:29:37.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000243)  
The chicken produced at 119 peso per kilogram.

**[00:29:42.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000244)  
These figures highlight the important question.

**[00:29:45.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000245)  
Are we adequately recognizing the contribution of our farmers and fisher folks to the value generated across the economy?

**[00:29:55.300] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000248)  
We saw the sector importance during the 2020 pandemic when its share in GDP rose to 10.1%, largely because other sectors contracted more sharply.

**[00:30:07.640] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000258)  
When we look at this sector, share in GDP,

**[00:30:14.120] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000259)  
therefore, there is a risk at overlooking agriculture and fisheries in the economic priorities.

**[00:30:21.880] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000260)  
It bears to emphasize that agriculture is the foundational industry.

**[00:30:26.380] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000261)  
This is a bedrock upon which many other industries and economic activities depend.

**[00:30:34.020] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000262)  
Investing in agriculture, therefore, is not merely investing in the farmers and the fisherfolk.

**[00:30:39.500] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000263)  
It is investing in the strengths, resiliency, and growth in the Philippine economy.

**[00:30:45.920] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000264)  
As we have seen so far, agriculture and fisheries sector creates value,

**[00:30:51.720] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000265)  
yet food insecurity and poverty and vulnerability

**[00:30:56.380] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000268)  
yet food insecurity and poverty and vulnerability exist among the sector.

**[00:30:59.400] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000269)  
These persistent challenges can be faced to fundamental bottlenecks

**[00:31:04.520] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000270)  
across the agriculture and fisheries value chain,

**[00:31:08.560] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000271)  
beginning at the resource level where agricultural land is becoming increasingly fragmented,

**[00:31:15.960] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000272)  
with an average size failing from 3.59 hectares to just 0.82 hectares.

**[00:31:25.700] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000273)  
This suggests that the only way to move forward is to cluster these farms into economic enterprise

**[00:31:36.740] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000274)  
to allow what we call the economies of scale.

**[00:31:41.880] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000275)  
At the production level, high input costs and low mechanization continue to constrain productivity,

**[00:31:48.040] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000276)  
with major commodities falling short of their 2025 PDP.

**[00:31:55.700] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000277)  
At the post-harvest stage, we continue to lose significant share of what we produce.

**[00:32:04.160] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000278)  
Losses reach 14.5% for rice, 31.6% for onion, and 26.5% for mangoes, as an example.

**[00:32:16.860] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000279)  
This shows that the challenge is not only how to produce more, but to prevent losses and preserve them.

**[00:32:25.680] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000280)  
We need to increase the value of production all the way to the market.

**[00:32:29.220] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000281)  
Finally, logistics and market access remain constrained by inadequate infrastructure and weak transport system.

**[00:32:38.840] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000282)  
An estimated 55,050 km gap in farm-to-market access

**[00:32:45.360] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000283)  
and 68,433-pallet [unclear] deficit in cold storage capacity

**[00:32:55.080] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000284)  
to continue to

**[00:32:55.680] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000285)  
raise transport costs, increase losses, and limit farmers' access to market.

**[00:33:04.920] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000286)  
As mentioned by the Secretary, as if the challenge continues,

**[00:33:12.920] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000287)  
along with this existing challenge, the impact on the conflict in the Middle East,

**[00:33:19.660] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000288)  
particularly on fuel and fertilizer, has increased drastically.

**[00:33:25.680] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000289)  
A year-to-year comparison of August National Weighted Average of Fertilizer will show

**[00:33:35.340] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000290)  
a higher prices of 6 out of 7 major grades used.

**[00:33:43.500] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000291)  
The highest percentage increase among these includes 46.7% for granular urea,

**[00:33:51.880] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000292)  
43.61% for oil,

**[00:33:55.680] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000293)  
and 40.89% for pilled urea.

**[00:34:03.460] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000294)  
So ito po yung mga figures, sobrang laki na po ng itinaas.

**[00:34:09.860] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000295)  
Fertilizer prices began rising in the late 2020 as demand recovered from COVID-19,

**[00:34:16.520] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000296)  
while supply chain remained disrupted and energy cost increased.

**[00:34:22.780] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000297)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:34:25.680] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000298)  
The prices.

**[00:34:30.960] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000299)  
Okay, next slide, next slide.

**[00:34:34.760] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000300)  
Okay, that's okay.

**[00:34:38.940] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000301)  
We go to the farmgate prices.

**[00:34:40.800] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000302)  
We have already expounded on the price of fertilizer and fuel.

**[00:34:45.620] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000303)  
Now we go to, well, contrasting the cost of production is the year-on-year.

**[00:34:53.960] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000304)  
Okay.

**[00:34:54.060] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000305)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:34:55.160] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000306)  
Okay.

**[00:34:55.460] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000307)  
Go back to the previous slide right after the, okay.

**[00:35:04.580] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000308)  
Now, notwithstanding the cost or the increase in production cost this year,

**[00:35:12.280] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000309)  
a year-on-year comparison of June farmgate prices in selected commodities show varying price changes.

**[00:35:19.940] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000310)  
There is a notable increase in dry palay.

**[00:35:25.460] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000311)  
In bangus.

**[00:35:26.160] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000312)  
But we further noted a decrease in prices in hogs.

**[00:35:33.280] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000313)  
It's 19%.

**[00:35:34.580] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000314)  
In red onion, 69%.

**[00:35:38.400] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000315)  
In broiler, 9%.

**[00:35:40.180] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000316)  
Chicken eggs, 7%.

**[00:35:44.320] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000317)  
And tilapia, 3%.

**[00:35:46.280] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000318)  
So tumataas po yung cost of production, pero generally bumababa po yung presyo at the farmgate level.

**[00:35:52.980] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000319)  
Okay.

**[00:35:53.300] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000320)  
Particularly for hogs at this time.

**[00:35:59.940] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000321)  
The agricultural sector recorded a trade deficit of around 11.12 billion in 2025.

**[00:36:09.940] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000322)  
But it represents a modest improvement of about 5% compared to the previous year.

**[00:36:16.740] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000323)  
This aligns with the trend observed in 2022 where trade gaps are gradually narrowing.

**[00:36:22.300] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000324)  
Narrowing.

**[00:36:23.300] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000325)  
Supported by raising export alongside moderate import growth.

**[00:36:27.900] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000326)  
Despite this encouraging developments, the trade deficit remains substantial.

**[00:36:33.760] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000327)  
Bringing this gap will require sustained support for export expansion while aggressively bolstering local farm yields and domestic supply chain.

**[00:36:48.020] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000328)  
Looking at the composition of agricultural trade,

**[00:36:51.540] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000329)  
export remains substantial.

**[00:36:53.300] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000330)  
It remains heavily concentrated on just a few commodities.

**[00:36:57.300] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000331)  
Coconut products lead toward the average of about 3.6 billion, followed by bananas, at 1.68 billion.

**[00:37:08.300] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000332)  
Incidentally, Mr. Chair, we are now confirmed as the second biggest exporter of banana in the world.

**[00:37:17.080] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000333)  
And pineapple products, around 3.370 million.

**[00:37:24.400] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000338)  
On the import side, animal and vegetable oils and pot account for the largest,

**[00:37:29.820] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000339)  
followed by fruits and vegetables and animal feeds.

**[00:37:32.780] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000340)  
Together, these figures underscore the sector's narrow export resilience,

**[00:37:39.700] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000341)  
reliance and on the handful products alongside substantial widespread demand for foreign food items at critical production inputs.

**[00:37:52.020] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000342)  
Now,

**[00:37:53.220] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000343)  
Let me bring you to the sufficiency level of selected commodities.

**[00:37:58.280] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000344)  
Based on the 2025 self-sufficiency level,

**[00:38:03.420] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000345)  
domestic production is sufficient in several commodities,

**[00:38:07.160] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000346)  
including root crops and vegetables.

**[00:38:11.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000347)  
However, there remains a significant gap for rice, potato, onion, coffee, mango, garlic,

**[00:38:20.360] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000348)  
indicating continued reliance on imports.

**[00:38:29.060] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000349)  
At the livestock level,

**[00:38:32.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000350)  
livestock and poultry show generally strong domestic production

**[00:38:36.240] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000351)  
with chebon and eggs at 100% sufficiency.

**[00:38:41.840] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000352)  
Chicken and duck's meat are close to self-sufficiency,

**[00:38:45.100] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000353)  
while caribbean, pork, beef,

**[00:38:49.240] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000354)  
you know, import mix,

**[00:38:50.360] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000355)  
are a large supply of these commodities.

**[00:38:53.640] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000356)  
For fisheries, we have crabs, oysters,

**[00:38:58.140] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000357)  
but when it comes to milkfish, tilapia,

**[00:39:01.100] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000358)  
we are nearly 100%.

**[00:39:03.020] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000359)  
However, for tuna and round scud,

**[00:39:05.740] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000360)  
we remain below self-sufficiency.

**[00:39:10.400] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000361)  
Although the department's budget has expanded,

**[00:39:15.520] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000362)  
its share in the national budget remains relatively low,

**[00:39:18.840] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000363)  
averaging just...

**[00:39:20.360] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000364)  
2.62% over the past two decades.

**[00:39:25.640] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000365)  
While comparing it with our Asian neighbor,

**[00:39:28.980] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000366)  
FAO data underscores this gap,

**[00:39:31.520] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000367)  
showing that agriculture's share of government expenditure

**[00:39:34.560] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000368)  
in the Philippines is a lot lower than the expenditures in China,

**[00:39:40.820] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000369)  
India, Thailand, Vietnam, Bangladesh, and Cambodia.

**[00:39:45.280] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000370)  
Looking at Indonesia, by contrast,

**[00:39:50.360] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000371)  
Indonesia indicates a slightly lower share at 2.6%,

**[00:39:55.640] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000372)  
yet maintains a larger agricultural sector

**[00:39:58.280] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000373)  
that achieves rice sufficiency in 2025.

**[00:40:02.700] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000374)  
This comparison suggests that progress depends not only on spending,

**[00:40:08.520] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000375)  
but on effective resource management and productivity conversion.

**[00:40:12.220] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000376)  
A key factor that has been Indonesia's focus is agricultural extension since 2020.

**[00:40:20.360] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000377)  
Demonstrating that robust extension services serve as a vital mechanism

**[00:40:27.520] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000378)  
for technology transfer, farmer decision support,

**[00:40:31.100] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000379)  
and targeted intervention delivery.

**[00:40:35.800] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000380)  
Before we proceed, Mr. Chair, to FY2027 budget,

**[00:40:43.240] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000381)  
we would like to highlight the five key result areas

**[00:40:46.500] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000382)  
under the

**[00:40:48.080] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000383)  
Para sa Masabagmaganan,

**[00:40:49.360] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000384)  
and the Five Key Result Areas.

**[00:40:49.480] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000385)  
Under the Para sa Masabagmaganan,

**[00:40:50.360] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000386)  
we focus our plans and programs among these key objectives.

**[00:40:57.900] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000387)  
The first one is on enhanced productivity.

**[00:41:03.480] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000388)  
We need to increase the yield per hectare.

**[00:41:06.560] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000389)  
We cannot increase our land area,

**[00:41:09.220] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000390)  
but definitely we can increase the yield.

**[00:41:12.160] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000391)  
We need to increase income,

**[00:41:14.240] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000392)  
and that means lowering the cost and increasing the value.

**[00:41:18.880] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000393)  
We need to improve food security and proper nutrition.

**[00:41:23.760] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000394)  
It is not just producing food, but producing nutritious food.

**[00:41:29.100] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000395)  
We need to reduce our dependence on import by increasing or improving self-sufficiency,

**[00:41:37.940] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000396)  
and ultimately reduce poverty in the agriculture and fishery sector.

**[00:41:44.620] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000397)  
For the next set of presentation, Mr. Chair,

**[00:41:48.880] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000398)  
I would like to turn over the floor to Undersecretary Nora Oliveros,

**[00:41:54.800] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000399)  
who will cast the financial component of our report.

**[00:41:57.700] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000400)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:41:58.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000401)  
Go ahead, Yusek Oliveros.

**[00:42:01.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000402)  
Thank you, Yusek Aziz Perez.

**[00:42:04.040] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000403)  
You now have the floor, Yusek Nora.

**[00:42:06.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000404)  
Go ahead.

**[00:42:11.740] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000405)

**[00:42:13.680] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000406)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:42:15.440] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000407)

**[00:42:17.100] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000408)  
Before I proceed to the 2027 budget proposal of the Department of Agriculture,

**[00:42:24.960] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000409)  
let me first brief the Committee on the Utilization of the Funds of DA for 2026.

**[00:42:38.820] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000410)  
Well, this slide presents the utilization status of the Department of Agriculture,

**[00:42:45.260] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000411)  
net of attached corporation,

**[00:42:47.100] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000412)  
as of September 30, 2026,

**[00:42:50.580] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000413)  
covering both current and continuing appropriations.

**[00:42:55.980] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000414)  
The Department has received a total allotment of ₱141.5 billion

**[00:43:01.360] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000415)  
out of the ₱172 billion total adjusted appropriations.

**[00:43:07.780] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000416)  
Of this amount, ₱90.4 billion has been obligated,

**[00:43:12.960] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000417)  
representing an overall obligation rate of 63.9%.

**[00:43:17.100] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000418)  
Total disbursements amounted to ₱50.8 billion, equivalent to 56.2% of total obligations.

**[00:43:27.560] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000419)  
The majority of the allotment is under current appropriation with an obligation rate of 67%

**[00:43:34.160] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000420)  
and a disbursement rate of 58%.

**[00:43:37.620] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000421)  
For continuing appropriations, the obligation rate is 43.6%,

**[00:43:43.320] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000422)  
while the disbursement rate is 38%.

**[00:43:47.100] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000423)  
These figures are being monitored by the Department

**[00:43:50.320] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000424)  
and have instructed all heads of the agencies

**[00:43:54.680] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000425)  
to draw a catch-up plan for 2026

**[00:43:59.000] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000426)  
to ensure the timely utilization and disbursement of the available funds

**[00:44:04.240] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000427)  
in accordance with the approved programs and applicable government rules.

**[00:44:10.120] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000428)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:44:13.040] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000429)  
This slide shows the summary by major programs,

**[00:44:17.100] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000430)  
representing 82% of the total allotment received by the Department of Agriculture.

**[00:44:23.720] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000431)  
The Department of Agriculture's major programs are anchored by high-performing areas

**[00:44:28.880] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000432)  
like the Agricultural Credit Program,

**[00:44:31.660] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000433)  
which achieve highest obligation and disbursement rate.

**[00:44:35.920] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000434)  
Key initiatives such as the National Rice Program show strong fund commitment,

**[00:44:40.980] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000435)  
though disbursement acceleration is needed across several sectors,

**[00:44:45.840] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000436)  
Most notably

**[00:44:46.720] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000437)  
on foreign-assisted projects where

**[00:44:49.320] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000438)  
payments lag behind obligations.

**[00:44:51.220] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000439)  
Overall, programs with large

**[00:44:53.160] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000440)  
budget allocations, such as

**[00:44:55.220] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000441)  
locally funded programs continue

**[00:44:57.480] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000442)  
to drive the bulk of total spending.

**[00:45:00.000] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000443)  
We have also an update on the unreleased funds for 2027, one reason why we have low obligation rates and disbursements.

**[00:45:13.280] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000444)

**[00:45:18.340] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000445)

**[00:45:48.340] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000446)

**[00:47:31.400] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000456)

**[00:48:33.420] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000466)

**[00:48:41.420] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000467)  
Now, may I go to the 2027 features of the DA proposal?

**[00:48:52.400] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000469)  
For the agri-sector, which includes the DA, the National Irrigation Administration, and the Department of Agri and Reform,

**[00:49:01.400] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000470)  
the total NEP that we have received as of today is 261.7 billion pesos, of which 198 billion is allocated to the Department of Agriculture.

**[00:49:18.340] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000471)  
So this represents a decrease in the budget of the DA comparing with the 2026 GAA of around 17 billion pesos.

**[00:49:30.700] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000472)  
For the whole sector, it is a decrease of 35.3 billion pesos.

**[00:49:38.700] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000473)  
Let me go to the details of the 198.4 billion allocated for DA for 2027.

**[00:49:45.300] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000474)  
The Department of Agriculture.

**[00:49:48.340] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000475)

**[00:49:58.160] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000476)  
The Department's budget is distributed across key expenditure categories.

**[00:50:04.240] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000477)

**[00:50:10.680] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000478)

**[00:50:18.340] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000479)

**[00:50:27.000] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000480)

**[00:51:03.300] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000483)  
So the operations budget is allocated around 156 billion for 2027.

**[00:51:11.580] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000484)

Okay.

**[00:53:16.420] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000501)  
Okay.

**[00:53:39.860] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000505)

**[00:54:06.420] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000511)  
Okay.

For the National Livestock Program,

The budget allocation for mainstreaming.

Climate Resilient Agriculture in Regional Programs and Projects,

**[00:55:02.580] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000522)

**[00:55:07.220] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000523)

**[00:55:18.400] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000525)

**[00:55:25.700] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000527)

**[00:55:38.940] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000529)

**[00:55:39.760] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000530)

**[00:55:43.020] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000531)

**[00:55:45.640] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000533)

**[00:55:58.020] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000537)

**[00:56:37.280] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000542)  
lahat din po ito ay nagkaroon ng decrease.

**[00:56:40.140] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000543)

**[00:56:43.180] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000544)

**[00:56:46.020] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000545)  
yung ating French Government Assisted Project Farm-to-Market Bridge Program,

**[00:56:51.120] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000546)  
which aims to address the continuing need for reliable and accessible bridge infrastructure in agri-production areas.

**[00:56:59.100] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000547)  
All others po ay decrease po ang ating na-receive.

**[00:57:06.040] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000548)  
Okay.

**[00:57:07.040] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000549)  
So I think that's it, Mr. Chair, for the features of the DA recommended by,

**[00:57:13.180] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-000550)  
and the budget for 2027.

**[00:57:19.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000551)  
I understand that you will end your presentation here,

**[00:57:23.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000552)  
and we have this understanding that we will not have a agency-by-agency presentation of budget,

**[00:57:31.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000553)  
but of course, all these attached agencies, the bureaus should be ready to answer the questions to be raised by our senators.

**[00:57:42.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000554)  
So,

**[00:57:43.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000555)  
with that, we can now proceed with the questions of our members of the Senate,

**[00:57:51.440] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000556)  
with the permission of our Senate President, Senator Aimee Marcos,

**[00:57:55.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000557)  
who was here 15 minutes before 10,

**[00:58:00.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000558)  
had requested that she be allowed to go for her first round.

**[00:58:06.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000559)  
And we will give each senator 10 minutes for their first round questions.

**[00:58:13.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000560)  
So, we can now proceed with Senator Aimee Marcos.

**[00:58:17.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000561)  
You have your 10 minutes.

**[00:58:18.780] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000562)  
Go ahead, ma'am.

**[00:58:20.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000563)  
Thank you very much, Chairman.

**[00:58:21.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000564)  
It's farming hours.

**[00:58:23.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000565)  
We wake up very early.

**[00:58:26.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000566)  
My question simply is, how much are we actually spending on rice,

**[00:58:33.620] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000567)  
and still persisting as the number one world's importer of rice?

**[00:58:40.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000568)  
The National Rice Program is 29.2%.

**[00:58:43.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000569)  
RCEP 30 billion, is this correct? RICE for all, 10 billion. Another NFA, 11.1 billion. This doesn't even count the Philippine RICE Institute.

**[00:58:57.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000570)  
ASEP, CEA, the Remedies Fund, CEMF, many support programs. OSEC, 44.2 billion for RICE again.

**[00:59:08.920] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000571)  
And then, parts that are for RICE under PRDP and the other foreign-assisted projects.

**[00:59:16.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000572)  
Pag sinumatotal po natin ito, hihigit po ito ng 81 billion, yung direct RICE lang.

**[00:59:25.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000573)  
Ano ba talaga ang dahilan na nagkukulang pa rin tayo sa pagbibigay ng kanin, ng RICE sa ating mga kababayan?

**[00:59:35.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000574)  
Kulang pa ba ito?

**[00:59:38.140] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000575)  
Mr. Chair.

**[00:59:38.920] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000576)  
Mr. Chair, from our records, it's 60 billion. Sa RICE, not 80 billion.

**[00:59:47.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000577)  
But yung sa 60, sorry Secretary, para lang sa kwenta. Sinasabi ninyong 60 billion kasi yung National RICE Program, halos 30. Tapos yung Regular RICE Program, 30 ulit.

**[01:00:00.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000578)  
60? Ganun po ba?

**[01:00:04.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000579)  
RCEP plus national rice.

**[01:00:06.460] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000580)  
Yung sa 60 billion,

**[01:00:08.040] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000581)  
Mr. Chair, is

**[01:00:09.100] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000582)  
yan yung direct sa

**[01:00:11.900] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000583)  
production. Kasi meron tayong

**[01:00:14.100] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000584)  
rice for all na 10 billion.

**[01:00:15.900] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000585)  
O, may 10.

**[01:00:17.020] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000586)  
Plus NFA na 9 billion, which has

**[01:00:19.920] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000587)  
nothing to do with production.

**[01:00:22.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000588)  
That's right. Tapos may presidential

**[01:00:23.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000589)  
10 billion rent for rice

**[01:00:26.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000590)  
assistant.

**[01:00:27.410] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000590-split-2)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[01:00:27.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000591)  
So, marami talaga

**[01:00:29.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000592)  
kapag sinumatotal?

**[01:00:31.920] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000593)  
Marami naman siya, pero kung

**[01:00:33.500] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000594)  
tinatanong niyong kulang,

**[01:00:35.300] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000595)  
ang sagot ko dyan, kulang.

**[01:00:37.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000596)  
Kulang. Ano ang dapat

**[01:00:39.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000597)  
para hindi na tayo

**[01:00:41.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000598)  
number one? After all,

**[01:00:43.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000599)  
between Erie and Los

**[01:00:45.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000600)  
Baños, we essentially,

**[01:00:47.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000601)  
Filipinos taught the world

**[01:00:49.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000602)  
how to plant rice.

**[01:00:51.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000603)  
Tayo naman nagturo eh. Bakit

**[01:00:53.380] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000604)  
nagkagalito?

**[01:00:55.160] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000605)  
Kung akong

**[01:00:56.180] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000606)  
tatanong niyo, number one,

**[01:00:58.860] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000607)  
irigasyon.

**[01:00:59.600] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000608)  
Kulang na kulang.

**[01:01:01.280] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000609)  
Sa irigasyon alone,

**[01:01:04.120] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000610)  
ang kailangan...

**[01:01:04.910] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000610-split-2)  
Pero yung irigasyon,

**[01:01:05.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000611)  
yung NIA, wala na sa inyo, hindi po ba?

**[01:01:07.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000612)  
Nasa OP na po?

**[01:01:08.760] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000613)  
Pero ang tinatanong niyo, kasi

**[01:01:10.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000614)  
bakit kulang tayo sa bigas eh?

**[01:01:14.520] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000615)  
Ang sagot ko dyan,

**[01:01:16.200] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000616)  
kailangan mag-invest po

**[01:01:17.380] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000617)  
ang ating bansa sa irigasyon.

**[01:01:19.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000618)  
And it will take 1.2 trillion

**[01:01:21.580] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000619)  
pesos to irrigate the remaining

**[01:01:23.500] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000620)  
lands natin.

**[01:01:25.500] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000621)  
As far as the DA naman is concerned,

**[01:01:27.920] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000622)  
Mr. Chair, we need

**[01:01:29.600] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000623)  
more money for post-harvest facilities,

**[01:01:32.060] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000624)  
especially dryers for our farmers

**[01:01:34.040] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000625)  
and

**[01:01:35.780] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000626)  
mechanization.

**[01:01:39.240] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000627)  
Magkano po ang tinitignan ninyo

**[01:01:41.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000628)  
para sa post-harvest?

**[01:01:43.040] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000629)  
Maybe another 50 billion, sir.

**[01:01:45.140] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000630)  
Ama, Mr. Chair.

**[01:01:46.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000631)  
5-0?

**[01:01:47.140] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000632)  
I mean, over the years, hindi naman yung immediate.

**[01:01:50.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000633)  
Sang-ayon po ako sa sinasabi ninyo,

**[01:01:52.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000634)  
talagang kulang ang

**[01:01:53.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000635)  
agricultural infrastructure investment,

**[01:01:56.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000636)  
itong irigasyon.

**[01:01:57.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000637)  
Sinasagot na rin natin sa PRD,

**[01:01:59.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000638)  
PPP, sa mga farm-to-market road,

**[01:02:01.660] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000639)  
at yung post-harvest nga,

**[01:02:03.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000640)  
na napakalaki ng ating loss

**[01:02:05.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000641)  
sa post-harvest.

**[01:02:07.480] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000642)  
So, napakalaki pa rin.

**[01:02:09.340] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000643)  
Solar irrigation din, under din sa amin

**[01:02:11.760] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000644)  
ang solar irrigation,

**[01:02:13.540] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000645)  
lalo ngayon, El Nino, kulang ang budget

**[01:02:15.660] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000646)  
natin para dyan, Mr. Chair.

**[01:02:17.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000647)  
Pero nakita ko na nagbawas kayo ng budget

**[01:02:19.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000648)  
kahit mag-EL Nino.

**[01:02:21.840] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000649)  
Mr. Chair, nagbawas kami ng budget

**[01:02:23.980] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000650)  
because hindi yan yung aming

**[01:02:26.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000651)  
ang proposal namin actually

**[01:02:27.620] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000652)  
mas malaki.

**[01:02:29.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000653)  
So, binagsak sa DBCC?

**[01:02:33.540] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000654)  
Yes, at

**[01:02:34.380] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000655)  
basically, we had to work

**[01:02:37.620] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000656)  
on a CAPE, Mr. Chair.

**[01:02:39.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000657)  
Palibasa tayo nga ang

**[01:02:41.440] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000658)  
importer, nagiging usapin

**[01:02:43.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000659)  
yung importasyon. For clarification,

**[01:02:46.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000660)  
who approves the importation

**[01:02:47.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000661)  
permits and clearances?

**[01:02:49.520] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000662)  
Hindi po ba yung Bureau of Plant Industry

**[01:02:51.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000663)  
under DA?

**[01:02:53.440] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000664)  
Tama po ba yan?

**[01:02:54.880] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000665)  
Yes, it goes through the

**[01:02:57.600] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000666)  
BPI, Mr. Chair.

**[01:02:58.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000667)  
That's right.

**[01:02:59.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000668)  
Yun din ang alam ko.

**[01:03:01.240] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000669)  
And under the present law,

**[01:03:02.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000670)  
which government agency is authorized

**[01:03:04.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000671)  
to import rice on a regular basis?

**[01:03:09.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000672)  
Wala, Mr. Chair.

**[01:03:11.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000673)  
The general rule is that

**[01:03:12.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000674)  
the government, especially

**[01:03:15.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000675)  
NTAs, prohibited from

**[01:03:16.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000676)  
importing rice. Di po ba?

**[01:03:18.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000677)  
There's a single exception

**[01:03:20.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000678)  
provided in RA 11203

**[01:03:23.660] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000679)  
Section 6.

**[01:03:25.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000680)  
Kasi talagang pinaghirapan po

**[01:03:27.040] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000681)  
namin yan na kinakailangan.

**[01:03:29.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000682)  
Yung emergency presidential powers

**[01:03:31.880] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000683)  
lang ang magsasabi kapag may

**[01:03:33.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000684)  
imminent or forecasted domestic

**[01:03:35.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000685)  
rice shortage. Tama po ba?

**[01:03:38.840] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000686)  
I would like to clarify

**[01:03:40.060] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000687)  
na hindi hong wala ang allowed.

**[01:03:42.400] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000688)  
Meron hong pwede.

**[01:03:44.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000689)  
Okay. Sinong po sa gobyerno

**[01:03:45.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000690)  
ang allowed?

**[01:03:46.980] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000691)  
PITC will be allowed.

**[01:03:50.320] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000692)  
FTI may be allowed.

**[01:03:52.440] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000693)  
Full terminal.

**[01:03:53.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000694)  
And FTI may be allowed

**[01:03:55.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000695)  
under

**[01:03:56.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000696)  
our understanding is

**[01:03:59.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000697)  
the DA can designate also

**[01:04:01.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000698)  
an entity to import rice

**[01:04:04.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000699)  
under G2G contracts.

**[01:04:06.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000700)  
Is that correct?

**[01:04:08.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000701)  
Provided it's not NFA.

**[01:04:10.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000702)  
Kaya yung sinasabi po ninyo, pag in-assign ninyo

**[01:04:12.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000703)  
yung PITC or yung FTI,

**[01:04:14.620] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000704)  
pwede naman sila. Tama po ba?

**[01:04:16.740] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000705)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[01:04:17.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000706)  
I have a question. Yung GOCC ba?

**[01:04:20.180] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000707)  
Authorized to import rice?

**[01:04:22.560] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000708)  
FTI is a GOCC.

**[01:04:24.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000709)  
So FTI and PICC

**[01:04:26.700] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000710)  
yung sinasabi rin ninyo?

**[01:04:28.040] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000711)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[01:04:29.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000712)  
Natanong ko kasi, nalilito po ako.

**[01:04:32.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000713)  
Since May,

**[01:04:34.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000714)  
matagal ko nang tinatanong

**[01:04:35.920] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000715)  
sa planters products

**[01:04:37.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000716)  
kung ano talaga ang status nila.

**[01:04:40.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000717)  
At nakatanggap ako

**[01:04:41.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000718)  
ng sulat mula sa planters

**[01:04:43.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000719)  
na ang pinipilit nila

**[01:04:45.920] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000720)  
ay private corporation sila.

**[01:04:48.240] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000721)  
When in fact, the reality

**[01:04:49.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000722)  
is there's an abject lack of clarity

**[01:04:52.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000723)  
on the status of PPI.

**[01:04:54.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000724)  
There's a Supreme Court case

**[01:04:56.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000725)  
sa PPI versus Fertifil

**[01:04:58.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000726)  
na yung

**[01:04:59.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000727)  
Supreme Court sabi, private corporation.

**[01:05:02.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000728)  
Kaya tama naman yung sulat

**[01:05:04.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000729)  
ng PPI. But that case

**[01:05:05.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000730)  
was decided long before

**[01:05:08.240] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000731)  
the Governance Commission of the

**[01:05:09.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000732)  
GOCCs. So as of

**[01:05:12.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000733)  
30 June 2026,

**[01:05:15.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000734)  
ang GCG

**[01:05:16.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000735)  
continues to classify

**[01:05:18.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000736)  
both PFI

**[01:05:19.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000737)  
and PPI as GOCC.

**[01:05:22.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000738)  
So is PPI

**[01:05:24.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000739)  
a GOCC? Yun ang lista

**[01:05:26.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000740)  
ng GOCC.

**[01:05:27.980] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000741)  
Mr. Chair, in our opinion,

**[01:05:29.600] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000742)  
no. It is not a

**[01:05:31.400] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000743)  
GOCC. If it is
