**[01:42:47.240] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001763)  
Tama po ba?

**[01:42:47.720] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001764)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[01:42:48.520] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001765)  
And how do, how...

**[01:42:50.180] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001766)  
Paano makakatulong itong PAF, in presidential assistance,

**[01:42:54.320] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001767)  
itong worst case scenario na 4 million farmers maapektuhan natin?

**[01:42:59.860] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001768)  
Well, Mr. Chair, yung presidential assistance to farmers and fisher folks,

**[01:43:04.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001769)  
that is basically cash assistance, direct cash assistance to our farmers who are affected.

**[01:43:10.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001770)  
Of course, sarami ngayon ang affected at 4.6 million.

**[01:43:14.160] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001771)  
Medyo maliit yan.

**[01:43:15.900] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001772)  
But nag-uusap po kami sa kabinete.

**[01:43:20.180] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001773)  
With other, like Secretary of DOLE, Secretary of DSWD,

**[01:43:25.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001774)  
na we're pooling now our funds para nga kung may tinamaan ng El Nino na hindi na talaga makatanim

**[01:43:32.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001775)  
at walang trabaho.

**[01:43:36.540] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001776)  
So may cash assistance, may food packs, may tupad,

**[01:43:42.200] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001777)  
or maybe canal making or land preparation.

**[01:43:45.420] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001778)  
So ganun ho ngayon yung diskarte namin.

**[01:43:49.560] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001779)  
And moving forward to address para hindi yung dekahunt kaming lahat.

**[01:43:54.440] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001780)  
So kung saan kailangan,

**[01:43:56.220] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001781)  
kagaya yung may El Nino sa Camarines Norte na may tinamaan na area na doon,

**[01:44:03.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001782)  
na binanggit namin kay Sec. Rex Gachelian,

**[01:44:07.020] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001783)  
nagpadala ko kagad ng assistance ang DSWD in forms of cash din sa kanya dahil meron silang pondo.

**[01:44:13.880] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001784)  
Kasi mas mabilis mag-respond at mag-replenish ang DSWD.

**[01:44:18.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001785)  
So parang ganun na ho yung...

**[01:44:19.560] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001786)  
While 10 billion na PATH na ninalagay natin namin for 2027 sa direct cash assistance,

**[01:44:29.520] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001787)  
meron na kami access ngayon sa DSWD through them,

**[01:44:34.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001788)  
tsaka sa DOLI rin.

**[01:44:35.440] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001789)  
So yun ho yung mas magandang sistema namin ngayon.

**[01:44:38.980] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001790)  
Later on, meron kaming ginawang special committee for economic recovery.

**[01:44:46.700] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001791)  
And we included, si Majority Leader Migs,

**[01:44:49.560] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001792)  
include niya na rin doon yung El Nino.

**[01:44:52.800] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001793)  
So hingi kami ng detailed briefing on the government's response to El Nino.

**[01:44:58.520] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001794)  
Kasi...

**[01:44:59.840] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001795)  
padating na siya eh.

**[01:45:00.760] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001796)  
And it can be as worse as the Iran conflict in which yung presyo ng langis tumaas.

**[01:45:09.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001797)  
Ito naman, triple whammy. Wala ka ng trabaho, wala ka ng output, wala pa tayong makakain.

**[01:45:16.800] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001798)  
So we really need to make sure na ang gobyerno ay handa pagdating sa El Nino.

**[01:45:23.700] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001799)  
And so far, narinig ko kanina sa inyong briefing na meron na kayong parang whole of government approach.

**[01:45:30.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001800)  
To conserve resources between DOLE, DSWD, and DA.

**[01:45:35.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001801)  
Ganun ba ang approach, Secretary, to itong mga vulnerable sectors?

**[01:45:40.480] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001802)  
Yes, Mr. Chair. Not only that, pati sa DOH, meron din tayong collaborative with DOLE, DOH, DSWD, and maybe other agencies pa.

**[01:45:52.380] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001803)  
OCD pala, pati sa OCD under the DND.

**[01:46:00.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001804)  
Aside from PAF, what other components in the budget na gagamitin ninyo to respond to El Nino?

**[01:46:09.400] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001805)  
Meron din tayo, Mr. Chair, fuel assistance, of course, some fertilizer support, seed support, and transport support, no?

**[01:46:19.760] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001806)  
Yung logistic support na, like sa tollways, meron tayong mga agreement, and hopefully we can get more funding for that, among others, Mr. Chair.

**[01:46:29.340] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001807)  
Thank you.

**[01:46:30.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001808)  
So that's embedded already in the budget ng DA?

**[01:46:33.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001809)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[01:46:34.620] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001810)  
Thank you, thank you.

**[01:46:36.200] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001811)  
And on a detailed submission na lang, pakisubmit na lang, Secretary, yung DA's response to the El Nino.

**[01:46:47.060] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001812)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[01:46:47.900] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001813)  
I'll jump to another topic, Secretary, and ito na pag-usapan natin last year, yung farm-to-market roads.

**[01:46:55.980] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001814)  
And for 2026, yung farm-to-market roads.

**[01:47:00.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001815)  
Yung farm-to-market roads, nabigay, o tinransfer namin ni Senator Kiko, siya being the chairman of the budget of the DA, to the Department of Agriculture instead of the Department of Public Works.

**[01:47:16.540] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001816)  
But nakita ko dito sa utilization for 2026, hindi pa siya nai-utilize to date.

**[01:47:24.080] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001817)  
So, may we get a briefing on...

**[01:47:30.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001818)  
Tapos yung concept na yun, tinransfer natin sa DA, and then second, base dito sa aming report, doon sa 33 billion na farm-to-market roads, wala pang nagagawa for 2026.

**[01:47:44.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001819)  
Before you answer, just to place on record, we'd like to acknowledge the presence of our minority leader, Senator Alan Cayetano.

**[01:47:52.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001820)  
Thank you.

**[01:47:53.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001821)  
Go ahead, please.

**[01:47:54.900] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001822)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[01:47:57.540] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001823)  
Yung sa FMR...

**[01:48:00.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001824)  
May transfer na ho sa amin yan, at may mga conditions under the GAAD for the transfer that we had to set up several offices, you know, get more people together.

**[01:48:11.380] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001825)  
May delay kami sa requesting of SARO.

**[01:48:14.560] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001826)  
But of course, na-request naman yung SARO namin, I think, by mid-year this year.

**[01:48:20.840] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001827)  
But then, yung pera, dumating din lang September.

**[01:48:24.820] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001828)  
At then, nung binigay sa amin yung pera ng September,

**[01:48:30.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001829)  
it's an end of a quarter that we had to give it back, no?

**[01:48:34.380] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001830)  
Yung pondo.

**[01:48:36.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001831)  
At inire-release lang ulit ngayon yung pondo sa amin this October.

**[01:48:40.360] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001832)  
But on the positive note naman, Mr. Chair, we're ready to bid now.

**[01:48:47.420] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001833)  
At finally, matutuloy na ho yung implementation ng FMR natin for 2026.

**[01:48:53.920] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001834)  
And for 2027, pag na-approve na ho sa BICAM na yan,

**[01:48:58.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001835)  
we will be doing...

**[01:49:00.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001836)  
pre-procurement basis sa NEP and short of award

**[01:49:08.440] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001837)  
so that magmapabilis namin yung implementation yan sa 2027 also, Mr. Chair.

**[01:49:14.940] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001838)  
Itong 33 billion na FMR, kaka-transfer lang sa inyo, kaka-release lang ng SARO itong September?

**[01:49:24.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001839)  
Actually, ang na-release lang sa amin is 4.2 billion, Mr. Chair.

**[01:49:28.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001840)  
4.2 billion.

**[01:49:30.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001841)  
So may...

**[01:49:30.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001842)  
Nung September.

**[01:49:33.040] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001843)  
Tapos binalik namin yan, we had to return it dahil may end of the month cut-off kami.

**[01:49:38.420] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001844)  
Then binalik naman sa amin by this October lang yung 18 billion out of the 33, Mr. Chair.

**[01:49:44.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001845)  
Okay.

**[01:49:45.700] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001846)  
So magkano na ang na-release for 2026 lang?

**[01:49:49.040] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001847)  
18...

**[01:49:49.880] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001848)  
23 billion na pala.

**[01:49:52.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001849)  
23 billion. Okay.

**[01:49:53.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001850)  
And doon sa special provisions in which si Sen. Kiko ang nagsulat nun,

**[01:50:00.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001851)  
may mga requirements, for example, yung transparency, ilalagay sa portal, ilalagay sa website.

**[01:50:09.120] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001852)  
Ito ba ay naka-ayos na?

**[01:50:12.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001853)  
Mr. Chair, naka-setup na ho at ready to go.

**[01:50:16.700] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001854)  
So now, the challenge, nandito na yung challenge namin na maayos to at nakita na lahat ng tao how we perform.

**[01:50:24.600] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001855)  
And I have required actually our team sa DA to give a monthly report.

**[01:50:29.920] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001856)  
To me, on the status of each and every project with pictures, which I will also copy the committee chairman, Mr. Chair.

**[01:50:39.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001857)  
In fact, during the last hearing, it was a challenge to you.

**[01:50:42.840] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001858)  
Dahil doon sa DPWH, iba-iba yung presyo.

**[01:50:46.980] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001859)  
But I remember during that time, you said the DA can construct it more efficiently and much cheaper through the BAFE.

**[01:50:59.280] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001860)  
Ito ba ang BAFE?

**[01:50:59.900] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001861)  
I, ano ba ito, na-increase ba yung capacity nila?

**[01:51:05.800] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001862)  
Kasi yun rin yung isang worry namin, baka mabulunan yung BAFE, DA BAFE.

**[01:51:12.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001863)  
Have you already capacitated yung BAFE?

**[01:51:15.400] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001864)  
Well, we have increased its capacity but not enough, Mr. Chair.

**[01:51:20.620] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001865)  
But it will be a challenge.

**[01:51:24.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001866)  
It's not, hindi ito magiging madali but we will do our best.

**[01:51:28.780] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001867)  
We assigned a special undersecretary just to look into all of this team para may nakatutok na 100% so that we do it properly.

**[01:51:43.480] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001868)  
Yung sa presyo naman, at that time, we can make it lower.

**[01:51:48.340] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001869)  
As of now, bumalik kami sa original considering naman na prices of everything has gone.

**[01:51:58.780] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001870)  
Much, much higher.

**[01:52:00.420] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001871)  
But we were able to maintain yung dating presyo at 15 million per kilometer

**[01:52:06.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001872)  
which even yung contractor, sinasabi, medyo maliit na yung margin nila

**[01:52:10.900] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001873)  
but payag naman na kailangan lahat ng trabaho at kailangan ng projects naman ng mga contractors.

**[01:52:19.700] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001874)  
So for normal, on a normal condition, 15 million per kilometer pa ba ang cost?

**[01:52:28.620] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001875)  
Nang farm-to-market roads?

**[01:52:30.600] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001876)  
Sa ngayon, yun ang tamang cost na yun.

**[01:52:32.500] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001877)  
Yun ang tamang cost.

**[01:52:33.600] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001878)  
Of course, as you inspected, may iba nasa Bangil, di ba?

**[01:52:39.880] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001879)  
But on the average, assuming all things equal, 15 million ang cost.

**[01:52:43.100] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001880)  
Yan yung average, Mr. Chair.

**[01:52:44.620] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001881)  
Yan ang average.

**[01:52:45.700] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001882)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[01:52:47.440] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001883)  
I'll just ask some more questions on my second round.

**[01:52:51.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001884)  
Yes, thank you.

**[01:52:54.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001885)  
Senator JV, Chairman of the Committee,

**[01:52:58.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001886)  
please, you have the floor.

**[01:53:01.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001887)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:53:02.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001888)  
Good morning to Secretary Laurel, the DA family.

**[01:53:07.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001889)  
This was asked earlier, no, ng ating po, Senate President,

**[01:53:10.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001890)  
yung tungkol po sa Presidential Assistance to Farmers and Fisher Folk Program,

**[01:53:17.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001891)  
being the Chair of the Committee on Finance,

**[01:53:19.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001892)  
I noticed, as pointed out by our Senate President,

**[01:53:23.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001893)  
wala po ito sa NEP, but it was included in the House,

**[01:53:28.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001894)  
HGAB, or General Appropriations Bill from the House,

**[01:53:31.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001895)  
sa 10 billion.

**[01:53:33.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001896)  
Just want to ask, did the agency request it for this?

**[01:53:40.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001897)  
O ang house ang naglagay nito?

**[01:53:45.020] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001898)  
Yes.

**[01:53:46.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001899)  
Nung pinapareho namin yung aming NEP, Mr. Chair,

**[01:53:50.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001900)  
we were given kasi a cap, fiscal cap.

**[01:53:54.940] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001901)  
So, hindi namin sinama yung PAF initially,

**[01:53:58.620] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001902)  
sa NEP request namin,

**[01:54:00.760] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001903)  
but sa Congress, even actually sa Senate,

**[01:54:06.280] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001904)  
i-request namin talaga na may dagdag yan,

**[01:54:09.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001905)  
dahil nga, it's the looming El Nino.

**[01:54:11.920] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001906)  
So, kung madadagdagan pa yan, would be better,

**[01:54:14.620] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001907)  
but then, pwede rin naman dagdagan yun sa DSWD,

**[01:54:18.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001908)  
o kaya doon sa TUPAD,

**[01:54:20.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001909)  
that's unnecessary sa amin,

**[01:54:21.660] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001910)  
kasi nga, now we're working together naman eh.

**[01:54:24.140] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001911)  
But we really have to prepare for El Nino,

**[01:54:26.500] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001912)  
lalo na sa ating farmers and fisher folk.

**[01:54:28.620] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001913)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:54:29.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001914)  
Well, actually, when I first reviewed the HGAB,

**[01:54:34.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001915)  
nakita ko kasi, I got worried that the fund for the NDRMC,

**[01:54:40.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001916)  
for the calamity fund, natanggal na 9.5 billion,

**[01:54:44.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001917)  
but I saw this is a new entry.

**[01:54:47.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001918)  
But when you said also na tingin din namin na para naman ito siguro,

**[01:54:55.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001919)  
direct assistance to farmers and fisher folk,

**[01:54:57.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001920)  
in anticipation of El Nino, okay din kami.

**[01:55:00.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001921)  
And I agree with the House on this matter especially,

**[01:55:05.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001922)  
pero doon sa iba, medyo may mga ayusin pa namin,

**[01:55:09.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001923)  
may hindi kami isang ayon,

**[01:55:10.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001924)  
but with this, with your endorsement also,

**[01:55:14.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001925)  
tsaka kanina, napaliwanag yun naman kung paano nyo ito gagamitin,

**[01:55:18.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001926)  
in anticipation of the super El Nino.

**[01:55:23.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001927)  
Although NIA is not anymore under the Department of Agriculture,

**[01:55:26.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001928)  
we had the...

**[01:55:27.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001929)  
we already heard their budget.

**[01:55:30.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001930)  
Ang earlier you mentioned,

**[01:55:32.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001931)  
si Katari, nabanggit mo kanina yung ano, no?

**[01:55:34.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001932)  
More irrigation is needed.

**[01:55:36.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001933)  
But I...

**[01:55:37.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001934)  
I...

**[01:55:38.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001935)  
I propose to NIA to concentrate more on water impounding

**[01:55:43.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001936)  
and retention projects in the coming years, no?

**[01:55:50.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001937)  
Because every time, every rainy season,

**[01:55:54.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001938)  
sobra-sobra yung ulan natin.

**[01:55:56.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001939)  
Kaya lang,

**[01:55:57.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001940)  
toyo talagang walang-wala.

**[01:55:59.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001941)  
So, parang sabi ko,

**[01:56:00.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001942)  
if we continue on doing...

**[01:56:02.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001943)  
on adding more irrigation,

**[01:56:05.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001944)  
makadals and others,

**[01:56:08.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001945)  
pero kung wala naman dadaloy na tubig, sayang din.

**[01:56:11.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001946)  
So, yun lang.

**[01:56:13.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001947)  
While you just like to ask for your opinion or position on this,

**[01:56:18.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001948)  
do you think this will be a better choice?

**[01:56:20.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001949)  
Because I think we'll be hitting two birds with one stone here.

**[01:56:23.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001950)  
We'll be providing irrigation.

**[01:56:24.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001951)  
We will be mitigating flood also.

**[01:56:27.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001952)  
So, it will be for flood mitigation.

**[01:56:31.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001953)  
At the same time, we will have the needed water during the dry days,

**[01:56:35.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001954)  
especially now na meron tayo mga...

**[01:56:37.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001955)  
Yung ilin ninyo na, as mentioned earlier,

**[01:56:39.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001956)  
nagiging every three years na.

**[01:56:41.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001957)  
Instead of the usual, sabi nyo kanina, ten years,

**[01:56:43.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001958)  
nagiging five years.

**[01:56:44.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001959)  
So, would you also endorse or suggest that we pursue that direction

**[01:56:53.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001960)  
of having more impounding, having more...

**[01:56:57.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001961)  
dams in water retention facilities?

**[01:57:00.140] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001962)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[01:57:03.280] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001963)  
Along that, pati aquaculture will thrive, sir.

**[01:57:06.940] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001964)  
Definitely, nung pumasok ako, Mr. Chair, sa DA,

**[01:57:11.380] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001965)  
I already mentioned to many na

**[01:57:14.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001966)  
we are actually throwing away water.

**[01:57:18.720] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001967)  
Ang lipis ho ng Pilipinas.

**[01:57:21.800] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001968)  
Kung ang Palawan, mas manipis pa.

**[01:57:25.100] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001969)  
Kung humulan ho sa gitna ng Palawan,

**[01:57:27.720] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001970)  
ang tubig na ulan na yun, in 12 hours na sa dagat na.

**[01:57:33.480] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001971)  
Sa Luzon naman, ang calculation ko,

**[01:57:36.100] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001972)  
kung humulan sa gitna ng Luzon, kung may linya sa gitna,

**[01:57:39.980] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001973)  
ng malakas, in 48 hours na sa dagat na yung tubig na yan.

**[01:57:45.220] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001974)  
Tapon ho yun.

**[01:57:46.340] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001975)  
Kung lalagyan ho natin ng halaga yung tubig na humulan

**[01:57:50.220] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001976)  
at napunta sa dagat,

**[01:57:54.120] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001977)  
I computed it at 500 billion pesos a year yun.

**[01:57:57.720] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001978)  
A worth of water ang tinatapon natin.

**[01:58:01.200] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001979)  
So, ang ganda ho ng inyong push, Senator JV, Mr. Chair,

**[01:58:06.600] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001980)  
and I fully support na kailangan talaga natin ng warm water impounding.

**[01:58:12.940] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001981)  
We have so much water to impound and we should impound it.

**[01:58:17.300] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001982)  
And NIA, in my opinion, should concentrate on the big ticket items

**[01:58:21.800] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001983)  
like dams, water impounding, and all the major canals

**[01:58:26.840] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001984)  
and water impounding.

**[01:58:27.720] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001985)  
Water waste for our agriculture, Mr. Chair.

**[01:58:31.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001986)  
Thank you for that, Secretary Laurel.

**[01:58:33.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001987)  
Likewise, even DPWH, Secretary Dizon,

**[01:58:37.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001988)  
I also suggested that instead of us concentrating on this flood mitigation project,

**[01:58:45.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001989)  
or dating flood control, flood mitigation,

**[01:58:48.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001990)  
para nangyari sisementohan lahat ng ano,

**[01:58:51.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001991)  
lahat na pwedeng simentohan nating water waste,

**[01:58:53.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001992)  
para mawala lahat ng tubig, ma-push back,

**[01:58:56.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001993)  
nangyayari,

**[01:58:57.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001994)  
wibilis lang yung takbo ng tubig para itapon.

**[01:59:01.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001995)  
Di ba?

**[01:59:01.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001996)  
Hindi pa na,

**[01:59:02.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001997)  
hindi pa na reprelish yung groundwater,

**[01:59:05.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001998)  
yung aquifer natin.

**[01:59:07.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001999)  
Di ba?

**[01:59:07.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002000)  
Nadideplete pa.

**[01:59:08.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002001)  
That's why we have sinkholes.

**[01:59:09.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002002)  
So, I think this has to be the,

**[01:59:12.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002003)  
dapat magkaroon ng convergence ang NIA and DPWH

**[01:59:15.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002004)  
so that we will have,

**[01:59:16.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002005)  
we will be preserving,

**[01:59:19.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002006)  
we will be retaining more water on dry days.

**[01:59:22.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002007)  
So, thank you for your opinion.

**[01:59:24.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002008)  
I'm happy that you support this,

**[01:59:27.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002009)  
and thank you for your support and for the direction.

**[01:59:30.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002010)  
Sabi ko kaya yung mga sabo dams,

**[01:59:33.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002011)  
dapat ganoon siguring gawin natin from the upland.

**[01:59:37.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002012)  
We are cascading,

**[01:59:39.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002013)  
mapabagal yung takbo ng tubig,

**[01:59:40.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002014)  
pero we retain.

**[01:59:41.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002015)  
Tama, may aquaculture,

**[01:59:42.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002016)  
maraming pwedeng gawin for tourism

**[01:59:44.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002017)  
and even for, of course, for irrigation.

**[01:59:50.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002018)  
And just like to assume,

**[01:59:52.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002019)  
because of our fiscal space,

**[01:59:53.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002020)  
very tightly in our fiscal space,

**[01:59:55.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002021)  
normally most of the agencies,

**[01:59:57.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002022)  
really suffered budget.

**[02:00:00.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002023)  
As Chairman of the Committee of Finance, siguro basic lang, Secretary.

**[02:00:07.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002024)  
Which of the programs would you appeal that be funded, na dapat ituloy?

**[02:00:15.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002025)  
Because yung halimbawa, pati bare minimum na rin na kailangan nyo sana,

**[02:00:20.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002026)  
I would like to find out, na increase in budget, kasi nga napakasikip.

**[02:00:25.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002027)  
Very, ano tayong elbow room natin, but then again, we would like to find out as Chairman of Finance,

**[02:00:30.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002028)  
na we do not want the productivity of the Department of Agriculture to be affected.

**[02:00:40.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002029)  
So siguro yung dalang, just briefly, which programs would you suggest that we really prioritize?

**[02:00:50.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002030)  
Siyempre, ganito naman yung budgetary, pang-appellate to sa mga tapyas,

**[02:00:54.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002031)  
para maidagdago natin, so that we would consider.

**[02:00:58.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002032)  
Anyway, the Vice Chair of Finance, Francis Pangilinan, will recommend, pero siguro,

**[02:01:05.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002033)  
but probably could already brief us today, kung ano po yung mga major programs that you think that would lead,

**[02:01:16.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002034)  
yung hindi pwedeng mawala, kung bagas sa inyong plano next, sa susunod na taon.

**[02:01:22.020] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002035)  
Mr. Chair, thank you.

**[02:01:24.720] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002036)  
Yan nga namin yung expanded fertilizer assistance of about 8.7B,

**[02:01:29.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002037)  
and yung mga hybrid solar-powered pump irrigation system,

**[02:01:35.300] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002038)  
irrigation systems for fish production, retarding basin, basically for water impounding din talaga,

**[02:01:41.880] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002039)  
yung mga SWIP projects namin.

**[02:01:44.840] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002040)  
Meron din kami yung request na sa masagana agri-food infrastructure modernization,

**[02:01:50.140] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002041)  
mga post-harvest facilities, dahil nga, lalo na pag El Nino,

**[02:01:54.720] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002042)  
may pagtataguan at madry at for milling, among others.

**[02:02:02.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002043)  
Marami pang iba, but we will submit the list, Mr. Chair,

**[02:02:05.580] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002044)  
para for your guidance and to the good Senator J.B. Ejercito. Thank you, sir.

**[02:02:10.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002045)  
Thank you for that.

**[02:02:12.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002046)  
Sige, we'll just, we'll review also, yung inyong listahan.

**[02:02:18.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002047)  
24 billion.

**[02:02:19.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002048)  
Wala, medyo 24 billion.

**[02:02:22.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002049)  
Yung mga, yung mga,

**[02:02:24.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002050)  
ang medyo malaki, pero we'll do our best, no?

**[02:02:28.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002051)  
Siyempre, ayaw naman nating mawala, no?

**[02:02:31.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002052)  
Because I think, yung itong Middle East crisis have taught us lesson,

**[02:02:36.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002053)  
na talaga, we really have to strengthen our food security and yung self-reliance, no?

**[02:02:42.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002054)  
And then, siguro, pwede din tayo magtanong sa, sa BFAR is here, BFAR,

**[02:02:47.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002055)  
Sir Saldy Perez.

**[02:02:48.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002056)  
Just like to ask, continues naman yung ating, yung ating po,

**[02:02:54.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002057)  
fisher folks, especially in the disputed areas, no?

**[02:02:58.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002058)  
Can you, ano, can you just briefly, ano na, kung paano yung operation po natin doon?

**[02:03:02.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002059)  
How many vessels are assigned?

**[02:03:06.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002060)  
How many personnel?

**[02:03:08.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002061)  
How many liters of gasoline are being distributed to our fisher folks in this area,

**[02:03:16.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002062)  
dun sa West Philippine Sea area?

**[02:03:18.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002063)  
Thank you.

**[02:03:24.720] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002064)  
To our, ah, Chair, Sir Francis Mangilinan.

**[02:03:33.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002065)  
Go ahead, Director Salilig.

**[02:03:35.240] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002066)  
Members of the Committee, Colleagues of the Government,

**[02:03:38.340] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002067)  
ang aming good secretary, ang gandang tanghali.

**[02:03:42.760] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002068)  
The Bureau of Fisheries, we continue to support to our, especially sa fuel subsidy,

**[02:03:51.240] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002069)  
ah, for...

**[02:03:54.800] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002070)  
piskalyer 2025 ah, namigay po kami ng 11,894

**[02:04:02.820] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002071)  
ah, na mga fisher folks po ang nakatanggap and then using fuel card, 11, 172 for fuel voucher.

**[02:04:13.520] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002072)  
Ah, we have seven hundred twenty-two and then ang target namin doon sa piskalyer 2025,

**[02:04:20.400] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002073)  
At least 23,504 na mga fisherfolk yung po ay mga motorized bangka.

**[02:04:28.100] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002074)  
In addition, nagbigay po yung ating magiting na presidente ng 1 billion.

**[02:04:34.820] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002075)  
Yun po ay nakabigay kami ng 11,172 na mga fisherfolk through using fuel cards and boat charts.

**[02:04:47.860] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002076)  
Until now, ang ongoing po ang aming distribution para ma-reach po ang ating mga target beneficiaries in addition to 105,126 fisherfolk beneficiaries are expected to receive our uplift funded fuel assistance program before the end of December 2026.

**[02:05:08.960] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002077)  
So, all in all, ang aming budget is sa mag-end of December 2026, we have 1 billion.

**[02:05:17.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002078)  
Ilang beses po ang ano dyan? May naka-assign yung beses dyan?

**[02:05:22.560] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002079)  
Yes, Mr. Chair. Mayroon po tayong...

**[02:05:27.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002080)  
Because with the continued harassment of our fishermen in the West Philippine Sea by the Chinese Coast Guard and militia,

**[02:05:39.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002081)

**[02:05:47.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002082)  
Yung period dyan.

**[02:05:55.860] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002083)  
Sorry, sorry.

**[02:05:58.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002084)  
Yung period dyan.

**[02:06:01.840] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002085)  
Mr. Chair, sanday lang ha.

**[02:06:04.580] BFAR Director Elizer S. Salilig** (asr-002086)  
Hinaarap ko pa yung...

**[02:06:09.500] BFAR Assistant Director for Administrative and Other Support Services Zaldy P. Perez** (asr-002087)  
With the indulgence of our National Director,

**[02:06:15.400] BFAR Assistant Director for Administrative and Other Support Services Zaldy P. Perez** (asr-002088)  
we deployed 30-meter vests.

**[02:06:17.860] BFAR Assistant Director for Administrative and Other Support Services Zaldy P. Perez** (asr-002089)  
And 50-meter vessels po in the West Philippine Sea.

**[02:06:21.400] BFAR Assistant Director for Administrative and Other Support Services Zaldy P. Perez** (asr-002090)  
And then we provided as well boats or fishing boats to our fisher folk for regions 1, 3, and 4B.

**[02:06:35.240] BFAR Assistant Director for Administrative and Other Support Services Zaldy P. Perez** (asr-002091)  
So, and then we provided po yung fuel subsidy for all those fisher folk operating on those area, Mr. Chair.

**[02:06:44.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002092)

**[02:06:47.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002093)

**[02:06:54.000] BFAR Assistant Director for Administrative and Other Support Services Zaldy P. Perez** (asr-002094)  
Yung fuel po namin, Mr. Chair, bali po, dun lang po sa available na pondo po natin.

**[02:07:01.740] BFAR Assistant Director for Administrative and Other Support Services Zaldy P. Perez** (asr-002095)  
But right now, it's good news po that we have this 1 billion pesos for our uplift to be distributed to our fisher folk po.

**[02:07:11.600] BFAR Assistant Director for Administrative and Other Support Services Zaldy P. Perez** (asr-002096)  
So, including po yung those fisher folk operating in regions...

**[02:07:17.860] BFAR Assistant Director for Administrative and Other Support Services Zaldy P. Perez** (asr-002097)  
1, 3, and 4B, Mr. Chair.

**[02:07:23.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002098)  
Pero yun pa rin ang ano, no?

**[02:07:24.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002099)  
That is still one of the richest in marine resources, to.

**[02:07:28.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002100)  
Kaya talagang...

**[02:07:29.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002101)  
Doon talaga nangingisahan to.

**[02:07:31.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002102)  
Pero is it true that, ano, almost 21,000 acres of coral reefs already are sa West Philippine Sea area?

**[02:07:38.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002103)  
Ano na, nasira na?

**[02:07:41.600] BFAR Assistant Director for Administrative and Other Support Services Zaldy P. Perez** (asr-002104)  
Do you have any news?

**[02:07:42.660] BFAR Assistant Director for Administrative and Other Support Services Zaldy P. Perez** (asr-002105)  
With that, Your Honor, we don't have the assessment yet.

**[02:07:45.600] BFAR Assistant Director for Administrative and Other Support Services Zaldy P. Perez** (asr-002106)  
But, of course, with the...

**[02:07:47.860] BFAR Assistant Director for Administrative and Other Support Services Zaldy P. Perez** (asr-002107)  
deployment of our vessel, we can...

**[02:07:51.780] BFAR Assistant Director for Administrative and Other Support Services Zaldy P. Perez** (asr-002108)  
pretty sure that we can assess the area.

**[02:07:53.940] BFAR Assistant Director for Administrative and Other Support Services Zaldy P. Perez** (asr-002109)  
But, of course, considering that there's no harassment at all, Mr. Chair.

**[02:08:01.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002110)  
Actually, I want to ask naman sa farm-to-market roads.

**[02:08:07.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002111)  
For 2013, the DA is proposing 16 billion for farm-to-market roads.

**[02:08:11.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002112)  
Bawat taon, iniulat kung ilang kilometre na kalsada ang natapos.

**[02:08:17.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002113)  
Sa kanatilang magsasakaba talaga ang nakikinabang.

**[02:08:20.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002114)  
So, taon-taon din kasi may mga balita pa rin tukol sa mga gulay at iba pang ani na nabubulok

**[02:08:25.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002115)  
at tinatapo na lang at hindi maibenta sa maayos na presyo.

**[02:08:30.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002116)  
So, malaki yung papel ng middleman.

**[02:08:32.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002117)  
So, question.

**[02:08:34.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002118)  
So, for next year, sa farm-to-market road,

**[02:08:38.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002119)  
how many kilometres is ang natin ang intended to construct for next year?

**[02:08:47.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002120)  
So, yung sa 16 billion, 16 billion na ba?

**[02:08:50.600] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002121)  
Mr. Chair, yung sa 16 billion na yun, 1,100, almost 1,100 kilometres, sir, ang farm-to-market road.

**[02:09:03.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002122)  
And, also, I'd like to touch on yung sa cold storage, Secretary.

**[02:09:11.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002123)  
In 2025, the cold storage expansion project reported disbursed only 1.4% of its

**[02:09:17.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002124)  
allocation.

**[02:09:18.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002125)  
So, of course, alam nyo naman ngayon, masikip.

**[02:09:22.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002126)  
So, we would, we want to make sure that this is all utilized and dispersed, yung mga pondo.

**[02:09:29.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002127)  
Ayaw natin maglagay ng hindi magagamit ngayon dahil nga sa sikip.

**[02:09:34.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002128)  
So, what caused the delays and how many cold storage facilities have actually been completed

**[02:09:38.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002129)  
and made operational?

**[02:09:41.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002130)  
So, siguro, yun muna.

**[02:09:43.020] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002131)  
I admit that we had a slow start.

**[02:09:47.860] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002132)  
Sa aming mga cold storage projects due to technical design issues.

**[02:09:54.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002133)  
But, fortunately, tamang-tama lang po yung tanong ninyo.

**[02:10:00.720] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002134)  
Tuloy-tuloy na namin inaugurate yung mga cold storage facilities na tinatayo.

**[02:10:05.560] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002135)  
Actually, I was just in Camarines Sur and we turned over four setups already.

**[02:10:16.500] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002136)  
Earlier this year, we inaugurated.

**[02:10:17.860] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002137)  
We inaugurated our first mega cold storage in Pili, Kamsur.

**[02:10:22.020] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002138)  
Yung sa Taguig na cold storage natin should be completed by middle of next year.

**[02:10:27.200] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002139)  
Cabanatuan will be, mega cold storage will be completed by next year.

**[02:10:32.340] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002140)  
San Jose, Mindoro, and ano pa isa?

**[02:10:36.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002141)  
Ang malaking bagay for the onions, ano?

**[02:10:38.020] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002142)  
San Jose, Mindoro, isang mega.

**[02:10:41.200] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002143)  
Tsaka, ano yung katabi niya?

**[02:10:43.620] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002144)  
Sa Sablayan, Mindoro also.

**[02:10:46.360] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002145)  
Sa Manolo Fortich.

**[02:10:47.860] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002146)  
Sa Sablayan lang, magtatayo rin tayo ng isang cold storage na malaki.

**[02:10:51.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002147)  
At total modular cold storage natin is about 70 units of which tuloy-tuloy na ho yung turnover niya.

**[02:10:59.760] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002148)  
So, pumilis na ho at I'm happy to note that medyo yung farmer beneficiaries natin quite happy na meron na silang solar powered and wind powered battery storage, Mr. Chair.

**[02:11:14.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002149)  
Well, that's good news for our local production, Secretary.

**[02:11:17.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002150)  
Let me move on to, ano, of course, that one, another sector that is close to my heart because coming from Negros, I saw Sir Paul Alcaza Ascona here, SRA.

**[02:11:31.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002151)  
My mother coming from, who hails from Negros, of course, sugar industry is very close to us.

**[02:11:39.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002152)  
SIDA has a mandated annual funding allocation under the law.

**[02:11:41.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002153)  
Yet funding has always fallen short. Under the 2027 NEP, 1 billion

**[02:11:47.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002154)  
is allocated to the SRA with the limited budget of SIDA.

**[02:11:51.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002155)  
How can we ensure that the full intended budget for SIDA programs is actually released and properly utilized to help our sugar cane farmers?

**[02:12:01.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002156)  
Siguro pwede nga na?

**[02:12:02.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002157)  
Si ating SRA administrator will take advantage of your presence.

**[02:12:09.120] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002158)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:12:10.680] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002159)  
Thank you, Senator JV.

**[02:12:13.080] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002161)  
Yeah.

**[02:12:14.340] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002162)  
So, so far, for the last...

**[02:12:17.860] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002163)  
Prior to 2022, our SIDA budget went down to as low as 300 million, 500 million.

**[02:12:25.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002164)  
Yes, I asked, pero 2 billion niya nung muna na.

**[02:12:27.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002165)  
Yes.

**[02:12:28.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002166)  
On the first year po, sir.

**[02:12:30.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002167)  
Because it was not utilized, it went down to 500.

**[02:12:32.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002168)  
Now, low as 300.

**[02:12:34.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002169)  
So now, it's back to 1 billion.

**[02:12:36.020] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002170)  
Yes po, Mr. Chair.

**[02:12:37.280] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002171)  
For the last three years po, 1 billion.

**[02:12:39.260] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002172)  
And then this year, yung alam ko po is 1 billion ulit.

**[02:12:43.540] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002173)  
We have...

**[02:12:44.620] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002174)  
We have carried over...

**[02:12:47.860] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002175)  
I think almost 500 million kasi na-unutilized ever since before.

**[02:12:52.760] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002176)  
And for the last three years, we've tried our best to spend yung previous po.

**[02:12:57.880] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002177)  
So, we've been spending over 1 billion for the last two years.

**[02:13:02.700] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002178)  
And hopefully, if given 2 billion, we will be able to fully utilize it, Mr. Chair.

**[02:13:10.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002179)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:13:11.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002180)  
We're hoping that it will be...

**[02:13:13.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002181)  
It will be used effectively and maximized.

**[02:13:17.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002182)  
Siyempre, tayo, ano eh, I'm the author of the Sugarcane Industry Development Act

**[02:13:22.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002183)  
with the intent of reviving the sugar industry of Negros.

**[02:13:29.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002184)  
Ang ano lang na, how about dun sa ano, how is the RSS affecting our sugar production?

**[02:13:37.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002185)  
Talagang minsan, hindi tayo inaabot ng swerte sa sugar industry.

**[02:13:40.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002186)  
Tinamaan pa tayo ng peste, no?

**[02:13:42.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002187)  
So, how is the sugar industry coping with it now?

**[02:13:47.860] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002188)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[02:13:48.620] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002189)  
So far, our production in 2024-2025 was...

**[02:13:55.620] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002190)  
Since 2022-2025, we've been growing, actually, in production.

**[02:14:00.780] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002191)  
Our peak happened in 2024-2025, we produced over 2 million tons.

**[02:14:05.960] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002192)  
And then, in 2025-2026, because of El Nino and the onset of RSS IPO,

**[02:14:14.280] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002193)  
we went down to 1.85.

**[02:14:17.860] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002194)  
And this year, in anticipation for the drastic decrease in rainfall on a year-on-year comparison,

**[02:14:28.160] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002195)  
our estimated production is down to 1.6 million.

**[02:14:32.500] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002196)  
And that's fighting RSSI as well.

**[02:14:36.540] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002197)  
RSSI po kasi, Mr. Chair, is not part of any budget, even SIDA.

**[02:14:42.300] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002198)  
SIDA kasi po is pre-allocated to certain items only.

**[02:14:45.680] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002199)  
And we hope and pray to the...

**[02:14:47.860] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002200)  
Committee and to the Chairman po, sana, that we can have additional budget to fight this new pest, RSSI,

**[02:14:56.760] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002201)  
which we never anticipated for Mr. Chair.

**[02:15:00.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002202)  
But how are we coping with it?

**[02:15:03.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002203)  
Meron na tayong solution for fighting the RSSI?

**[02:15:08.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002204)  
And also, were we able to provide assistance to the sugarcane farmers?

**[02:15:15.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002205)  
And how many in anticipation of the RSSI and may super-el-lino pa?

**[02:15:21.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002206)  
So that we can prepare already, syempre as budget chair, as finance chair, I would like to find out.

**[02:15:25.820] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002207)  
Yes. Mr. Chair, for the initial first year po of RSSI in Negros,

**[02:15:34.140] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002208)  
SRA from its meager corporate budget po spent about 188 million in assistance to farmers.

**[02:15:42.500] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002209)  
This includes chemicals, fertilizer, and others, among others.

**[02:15:47.800] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002210)  
And from SIDA, we were able to utilize about 28 million.

**[02:15:52.180] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002211)  
So it's a total of 209 million.

**[02:15:54.800] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002212)  
Now for this year...

**[02:15:56.780] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002213)  
The DA extended 35 million directly to LGUs

**[02:16:00.920] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002214)  
because we had issues on how to fight RSSI with the province and the other agencies.

**[02:16:07.900] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002215)  
So there's 35 million directly to LGUs.

**[02:16:11.800] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002216)  
And I think a total of 400-something million of assistance to farmers and farm workers po

**[02:16:20.700] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002217)  
at 10,000 per farmer or per worker po through DSWD po.

**[02:16:25.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002218)  
So, just a...

**[02:16:28.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002219)  
Anyway, I'm hoping that we'll be able to overcome this RSSI problem and super-el-lino.

**[02:16:35.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002220)  
But nonetheless, I'm also...

**[02:16:38.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002221)  
Yung commitment also of the SRA that the timing of the importation will now be...

**[02:16:46.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002222)  
Will not coincide with the harvest season.

**[02:16:50.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002223)  
So, kasi konti na rin ikikitain nila.

**[02:16:52.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002224)  
So, huwag na natin sasabayan.

**[02:16:54.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002225)  
Dapat na lang.

**[02:16:54.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002226)  
We understand.

**[02:16:55.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002227)  
of all of these factors

**[02:16:57.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002228)  
that we need to import also because of the

**[02:16:59.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002229)  
shortage but yung timing na lang

**[02:17:02.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002230)  
I'm hoping that we can get

**[02:17:03.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002231)  
your commitment that

**[02:17:05.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002232)  
SRA will

**[02:17:06.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002233)  
only issue

**[02:17:09.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002234)  
authority to import

**[02:17:11.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002235)  
not during harvest season.

**[02:17:14.720] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002236)  
Mr. Chair,

**[02:17:15.480] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002237)  
yes, I think

**[02:17:16.680] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002238)  
the Secretary po and the

**[02:17:19.400] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002239)  
SRA are committed to

**[02:17:21.520] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002240)  
use importation

**[02:17:23.440] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002241)  
on a per-need basis po.

**[02:17:25.820] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002242)  
It may or may not happen during harvest

**[02:17:28.200] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002243)  
season but we will only do it if

**[02:17:30.180] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-002244)  
we really need to do it po, Mr. Chair.

**[02:17:33.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002245)  
Thank you.

**[02:17:33.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002246)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:17:37.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002247)  
Thank you,

**[02:17:38.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002248)  
Senator JV.

**[02:17:40.200] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002249)  
We now have Senator Zubiri,

**[02:17:42.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002250)  
Senator Migs, our majority leader.

**[02:17:44.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002251)  
You have 10 minutes.

**[02:18:16.140] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002267)  
Mr. President, ah, Mr. Chairman,

**[02:18:18.560] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002268)  
halatang

magagandang mga, mababait

**[02:44:18.880] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003050)  
mahal dahil sa fuel

**[02:44:20.720] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003051)  
state of fuel prices.

**[02:44:22.880] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003052)  
But, with that said,

**[02:44:24.920] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003053)  
Secretary, what if there's a possibility na magkaroon

**[02:44:27.100] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003054)  
ng ban? Have you thought about coming up with

**[02:44:29.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003055)  
alternative avenues for

**[02:44:30.460] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003056)  
fertilizer? Oh, yes. Mr. Chair,

**[02:44:33.800] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003057)  
there's alternative

**[02:44:34.540] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003058)  
fertilizers, no? Of course,

**[02:44:37.220] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003059)  
chicken manure is one from

**[02:44:38.740] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003060)  
processed chicken manure, no?

**[02:44:41.400] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003061)  
Pati yung local industry

**[02:44:43.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003062)  
natin dito, medyo marami

**[02:44:45.360] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003063)  
ng gumagawa, no? That's the

**[02:44:47.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003064)  
positive side. And the DA is pushing for

**[02:44:49.140] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003065)  
that composting system

**[02:44:51.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003066)  
for chicken manure and

**[02:44:53.100] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003067)  
other

**[02:44:54.920] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003068)  
by-product ng ating mga

**[02:44:57.940] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003069)  
livestock sector. But then, we have

**[02:45:00.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003070)  
of foil air fertilizers, biofertilizers, and soil conditioners

**[02:45:04.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003071)  
na mas mura and equally as effective.

**[02:45:06.720] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003072)  
Kailangan lang tanggapin ng ating mga magsasaka

**[02:45:09.200] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003073)  
na these are the new alternatives that is available

**[02:45:13.940] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003074)  
at a lower price per hectare at mas makanda pa ang kita nila.

**[02:45:20.120] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003075)  
In fact, I have a personal farm na pinabili ko sa anak ko

**[02:45:25.100] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003076)  
at tinanimang ko ng bigasa.

**[02:45:26.800] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003077)  
Hindi ako gumamit ng urea.

**[02:45:28.020] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003078)  
And the margins they made were quite okay

**[02:45:32.040] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003079)  
using these alternative systems, Mr. Chair.

**[02:45:35.620] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003080)  
Thank you, Chairman, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:45:37.380] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003081)  
But aside from the alternative systems,

**[02:45:40.580] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003082)  
I think one of the reasons kasi pinapromote ko din

**[02:45:42.600] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003083)  
yung organic fertilizer, it's the volume, Secretary,

**[02:45:45.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003084)  
kasi kailangan mo sa isang bag ng urea,

**[02:45:48.860] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003085)  
kailangan mo ng isang tuneladang chicken manure.

**[02:45:52.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003086)  
Kaya yung mga farmers na iba,

**[02:45:55.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003087)  
wala silang capacity to put all these,

**[02:45:58.020] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003088)  
the large amounts of organic fertilizer

**[02:46:00.680] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003089)  
to be able to be spread out.

**[02:46:01.980] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003090)  
Pero I'm with you 1,000% because it is a soil enhancer

**[02:46:04.900] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003091)  
and it rejuvenates the soil.

**[02:46:07.660] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003092)  
But my question talaga is,

**[02:46:09.620] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003093)  
I think we should look into other avenues

**[02:46:13.400] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003094)

**[02:46:16.920] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003095)  
from other friendly countries like Indonesia

**[02:46:19.020] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003096)  
or Malaysia where they have petroleum products

**[02:46:24.900] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003097)  
kasi urea is a byproduct of petroleum.

**[02:46:27.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003098)  
So kailangan natin maghanap ng bagong avenues

**[02:46:30.440] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003099)  
just in case, Secretary, we never know where this escalates,

**[02:46:34.220] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003100)  
but just in case, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:46:36.900] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003101)  
So just a little information, point of information,

**[02:46:40.720] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003102)  
para we don't get caught off guard,

**[02:46:42.500] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003103)  
particularly on importation and inputs.

**[02:46:46.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003104)  
You know, yesterday, Mr. Chairman,

**[02:46:48.520] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003105)  
I had a talk with one of the members of the Central Bank.

**[02:46:52.220] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003106)  
My worry is there is no actionable,

**[02:46:55.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003107)  
there's no action on the,

**[02:46:57.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003108)  
increase on dollar rates.

**[02:46:59.520] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003109)  
Parang ang gusto yata ng Central Bank

**[02:47:01.980] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003110)

**[02:47:05.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003111)  
Delikado po yan, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:47:07.900] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003112)  
I think we should also, in the budget hearings

**[02:47:09.820] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003113)  
or deliberations of the Central Bank,

**[02:47:11.380] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003114)  
I would like to ask Banco Central Governor Remolino

**[02:47:14.260] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003115)  
na napakahirap yan because

**[02:47:16.640] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003116)

**[02:47:18.580] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003117)

**[02:47:21.640] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003118)

**[02:47:22.880] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003119)  
We cannot just promote exports biglaan.

**[02:47:25.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003120)  
We have to strengthen the industry.

**[02:47:27.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003121)

**[02:47:29.920] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003122)  
So for the meantime,

**[02:47:31.220] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003123)  
pabayaan lang po natin na

**[02:47:32.820] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003124)

**[02:47:35.660] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003125)  
to where it may against the dollar.

**[02:47:38.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003126)  
Kawawa po lahat ng kababayan natin.

**[02:47:40.220] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003127)  
Kawawa po.

**[02:47:41.420] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003128)  
From fuel, from gasoline,

**[02:47:43.320] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003129)  
from imported products,

**[02:47:45.340] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003130)

**[02:47:46.320] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003131)  
Pati sa agrikultura na yun,

**[02:47:48.660] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003132)

**[02:47:51.600] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003133)

**[02:47:53.020] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003134)

**[02:47:55.500] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003135)  
Lahat po ay galing Japan,

**[02:47:57.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003136)

**[02:47:59.400] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003137)  
Ford tractors.

**[02:48:00.320] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003138)  
Everything is imported.

**[02:48:02.220] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003139)  
Kaya naawa po ako sa ating mga magsasaka.

**[02:48:04.460] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003140)  
I think it's a perfect storm

**[02:48:07.380] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003141)  
as you said earlier on the El Nino.

**[02:48:09.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003142)

**[02:48:10.300] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003143)

**[02:48:13.620] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003144)

**[02:48:15.760] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003145)  
Tapos tumataas pa ang presyo ng gasolina.

**[02:48:19.160] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003146)  
Ang hirap.

**[02:48:20.140] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003147)  
Ang hirap po ng situation ng sekretary.

**[02:48:22.060] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003148)  
I don't blame you, Sec.

**[02:48:23.220] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003149)  
You know, pumapayat ka nga eh.

**[02:48:24.620] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003150)

**[02:48:26.120] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003151)

**[02:48:27.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003152)

**[02:48:29.040] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003153)

**[02:48:30.040] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003154)

**[02:48:32.040] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003155)  
So, pinapatawa lang po kayo, Sec.

**[02:48:34.700] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003156)  
I don't want to put you in that seat.

**[02:48:36.020] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003157)  
We've been friends for 35 years.

**[02:48:39.040] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003158)  
So, ang akin lang is,

**[02:48:41.360] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003159)  
I hope also that the House of Representatives

**[02:48:43.640] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003160)  
take note of the bills that we passed

**[02:48:45.520] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003161)
