**[02:48:48.700] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003162)  
I think the secretary needs that law to be passed

**[02:48:51.160] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003163)  
because the DA needs extension services

**[02:48:53.660] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003164)  
to really go to the ground

**[02:48:55.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003165)  
and be felt in the ground.

**[02:48:57.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003166)  
We cannot just...

**[02:49:00.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003167)  
Sorry na lang ha.

**[02:49:01.480] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003168)  
Makagalit sa akin yung mga PAO and mga MAO.

**[02:49:06.580] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003169)  
Municipal Agricultural Officers.

**[02:49:08.240] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003170)  
We need provincial agricultural officers.

**[02:49:10.460] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003171)  
The DA needs people on the ground

**[02:49:12.680] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003172)  
to tell them what is going on from the top.

**[02:49:15.800] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003173)  
What are the reforms being done on the ground?

**[02:49:18.900] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003174)  
Kailangan talagang nilang sundalo sa baba.

**[02:49:21.620] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003175)  
So, I just have one last if I may, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:49:25.320] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003176)  
Paano nila ako magsisecond round?

**[02:49:27.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003177)  
Yes, thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:49:29.760] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003178)  
Dito sa...

**[02:49:31.520] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003179)  
So, at least, we talked about alternatives on fertilizers

**[02:49:36.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003180)  
and inputs from other countries.

**[02:49:39.060] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003181)  
We have to discuss how we can also tame

**[02:49:41.340] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003182)  
the high inflation rates na talagang piktado

**[02:49:45.180] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003183)  
yung taong bayan natin.

**[02:49:46.760] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003184)  
Also, due to the fact of the falling peso to the dollar.

**[02:49:51.960] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003185)  
Some good news, Mr. Chairman, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:49:54.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003186)  
We passed the Kawayan Act,

**[02:49:56.580] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003187)  
the Bamboo Law,

**[02:49:57.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003188)  
and we passed the DPR Act.

**[02:49:58.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003189)  
We passed the DPR Act.

**[02:49:58.240] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003190)  
Finally, both House and the Senate

**[02:50:00.960] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003191)  
will be meeting during the break

**[02:50:02.380] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003192)  
to come up with an acceptable language

**[02:50:05.460] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003193)  
for the Bicameral Conference Committee report.

**[02:50:08.260] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003194)  
I think we're leaning towards the DA again

**[02:50:10.500] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003195)  
as being the number one source of motivation

**[02:50:17.900] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003196)  
and moral support for this law to pass.

**[02:50:21.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003197)  
Kasi matagal po yung away ninyo sa DNR, sa DTI, di ba po?

**[02:50:25.160] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003198)  
So, ang ginawa po natin, ako,

**[02:50:27.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003199)  
may bias to the DA because I'm also a resident agriculturist

**[02:50:30.900] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003200)  
and a licensed agriculturist at that.

**[02:50:33.180] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003201)  
We can now promote high-value farming

**[02:50:37.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003202)  
like with kawayan, bamboo.

**[02:50:40.760] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003203)  
Yesterday, I was in Bay Laguna

**[02:50:42.540] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003204)  
buying a bonsai, bogambilya.

**[02:50:45.440] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003205)  
Napaka-mahal.

**[02:50:46.900] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003206)  
At sabi ko nga, isang bogambilya lang na maganda

**[02:50:49.540] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003207)  
ay napaka-laki na ng income ng farmer na ito.

**[02:50:53.160] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003208)  
So, I think we should start looking into other

**[02:50:56.100] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003209)  
high-value companies.

**[02:50:57.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003210)  
We can also concentrate on plantation crops

**[02:50:59.940] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003211)  
for our farmers para maiba din po yung dumami din po ito,

**[02:51:02.960] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003212)  
lumakas po ang kanilang kita at benta.

**[02:51:05.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003213)  
So, I hope we can also concentrate, Sec,

**[02:51:08.040] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003214)  
not only on plantation crops like corn, like sugar,

**[02:51:12.820] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003215)  
like, of course, rice.

**[02:51:17.360] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003216)  
We can look into high-value horticulture.

**[02:51:20.860] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003217)  
Sana po buhayin ninyo pong mga department,

**[02:51:24.180] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003218)  
sa department, yung mga bureau ninyo ng horticulture

**[02:51:26.900] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003219)  
and high-value crops.

**[02:51:27.880] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003220)  
Nandyan si Usec Sheryl, high-value crops yan.

**[02:51:31.320] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003221)  
That is a shining light.

**[02:51:33.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003222)  
Alam mo paano ko nahanap po, Mr. Chairman,

**[02:51:35.700] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003223)  
yung bogambilya na yan?

**[02:51:37.280] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003224)  
Facebook.

**[02:51:38.400] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003225)  
Facebook lang.

**[02:51:39.180] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003226)  
Gagaling na nila mag-market.

**[02:51:40.640] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003227)  
So, pag nakita mo Facebook, nandyan yung address.

**[02:51:42.900] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003228)  
Puntahan ng mga buyers.

**[02:51:44.380] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003229)  
They told me they, because of Facebook market,

**[02:51:46.940] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003230)  
ang dami po nilang naibibenta.

**[02:51:49.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003231)  
So, there are other opportunities for farmers

**[02:51:51.480] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003232)  
with small areas that can make a lot of money.

**[02:51:54.960] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003233)  
So, we can look into that.

**[02:51:56.400] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003234)  
Because at the end of the day,

**[02:51:57.480] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003235)  
ang gusto po natin, taasan po ang kita ng ating mga magsasaka.

**[02:52:00.520] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003236)  
At hindi lamang po silang nakabinbin,

**[02:52:03.480] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003237)  
nakasalalay sa isang monocrop.

**[02:52:07.240] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003238)  
I hope that we can do that also, Secretary.

**[02:52:09.640] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003239)  
May we just ask for a comment from the Secretary?

**[02:52:12.620] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003240)  
Definitely, Mr. Chair, horticulture is on the agenda.

**[02:52:17.100] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003241)  
We have a small budget for that.

**[02:52:19.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003242)  
Plus, the DA, along with the DTI and other government agencies,

**[02:52:23.340] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003243)  
we're actually sponsoring sa Japan,

**[02:52:26.400] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003244)  
we will have a major booth in a horticulture show in Yokohama in March 2027.

**[02:52:33.800] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003245)  
Maybe may imbita ko kayo doon.

**[02:52:36.560] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003246)  
At I really see, personally see that horticulture is an alternative business

**[02:52:45.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003247)  
for our farmers and fisher folks.

**[02:52:49.380] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003248)  
Meron niyang mga plants na binibenta for 20,000 pesos, 10,000 pesos,

**[02:52:54.140] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003249)  
or kids man, o ganoon.

**[02:52:56.400] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003250)  
Even baka sa Benguet, pwede na magkaroon ng tulips,

**[02:53:00.020] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003251)  
yung type na ganoon, at magaganda pa.

**[02:53:02.560] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003252)  
Magandang pag-asa dyan.

**[02:53:04.280] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003253)  
Nakita ko sa ibang bansa na well-regulated and well-developed yan,

**[02:53:10.440] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003254)  
kaya tulad ng Thailand.

**[02:53:12.700] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003255)  
So gusto nating tularan yun at maging major industry din.

**[02:53:17.540] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003256)  
Thank you, sir.

**[02:53:18.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003257)  
Thank you.

**[02:53:18.580] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003258)  
Kasi ako, Mr. Chairman, may ilig ako sa ornamental plants.

**[02:53:21.180] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003259)  
So tama po sinabi yung Secretary, orchids.

**[02:53:23.960] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003260)  
As a matter of fact, namili po ako nung isang araw ng May,

**[02:53:26.400] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003261)  
maple trees, yung mga maple plants na yung nga lang mukhang mariwana,

**[02:53:32.540] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003262)  
baka marid bigla yung farm ko, pero hindi mariwana yun.

**[02:53:35.640] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003263)  
Maple trees yun na seedlings.

**[02:53:38.140] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003264)  
Ba, tagwa 1,500 din, kada isa, 1,000.

**[02:53:40.720] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003265)  
So laki din ang income.

**[02:53:42.440] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003266)  
So tama po si Secretary, there's a lot of things that we can do here in the Philippines

**[02:53:46.620] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003267)  
that can really augment the farmers, not only focusing on our plantation crops.

**[02:53:52.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003268)  
And finally, Mr. Chairman, so ayong information sa kawayan, Secretary,

**[02:53:56.400] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003269)  
malapit na po yan, baka matulungan niyo po kami.

**[02:53:59.640] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003270)  
We're leaning towards the DAS, the Implementing Agency for that SEC.

**[02:54:03.280] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003271)  
Okay lang po sa inyo yan.

**[02:54:04.440] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003272)  
We support that fully and I'm an avid believer in the bamboo industry and its benefits, Mr. Chair.

**[02:54:13.560] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003273)  
Thank you.

**[02:54:14.160] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003274)  
Oo nga, kasi yung pati nga airport namin sa Bukidnon, we're retrofitting it with bamboo inside.

**[02:54:19.720] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003275)  
Yung gagawin na yata, I think, in the Region 8 sa Tacloban Airport,

**[02:54:25.140] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003276)  
they already used bamboo.

**[02:54:26.400] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003277)  
Inside for the finishing.

**[02:54:28.540] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003278)  
So ginagawa din po namin sa Bukidnon yan with our Bukidnon Airport using Bukidnon bamboo.

**[02:54:33.560] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003279)  
So thank you, Secretary, for the support there.

**[02:54:35.360] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003280)  
We have actually a huge buyer in Cagayan de Oro.

**[02:54:40.760] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003281)  
I forget the name.

**[02:54:42.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003282)  
Yung kay Cito Lorenzo at Rizone.

**[02:54:45.280] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003283)  
They buy whatever they can produce there na bamboo.

**[02:54:48.660] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003284)  
At magagandang pong farmgate prices nila.

**[02:54:51.180] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003285)  
So marami po kaming farmers, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:54:54.160] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003286)  
Dati, ang subsistence farmgate.

**[02:54:56.400] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003287)  
Farmers lang po sila, corn and plant rice.

**[02:54:59.840] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003288)  
Ngayon, bamboo na sila.

**[02:55:00.880] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003289)  
They're making a lot of money out of the bamboo.

**[02:55:03.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003290)  
And tamas talaga yung kanilang livelihood opportunities, Your Honor.

**[02:55:07.540] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003291)  
So finally, I will be remiss to ask, if I don't ask this,

**[02:55:11.360] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003292)  
kasi po ako yung taga, of course, taga Bukidnon po ako,

**[02:55:14.160] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003293)  
pero originally taga Negro-Soxidental.

**[02:55:16.640] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003294)  
Kamag-anak ko yan, na malayo, si Paul Ascuna, yung inyong SRA chairman,

**[02:55:22.420] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003295)  
on the RSSI.

**[02:55:24.780] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003296)  
Kami natamaan din po kami sa Bukidnon.

**[02:55:26.460] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003297)  
We appreciate a lot of the work that SRA has been doing.

**[02:55:30.680] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003298)  
We're just seeking more support from the mother organization, the DA,

**[02:55:34.960] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003299)  
and the DSWD.

**[02:55:36.240] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003300)  
We'd like to thank Secretary Rex Gatchalian,

**[02:55:39.340] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003301)  
nagbigay po siya ng tulong sa mga farmers na natamaan ng RSSI.

**[02:55:43.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003302)  
Ito po, sa marami pong nanunood na yan,

**[02:55:45.300] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003303)  
at nakikinig, yung RSSI, ito po isang peste na tumatama po sa atin sa industriya ng tubo.

**[02:55:51.320] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003304)  
Bukidnon is the second largest producer of sugar in the country,

**[02:55:54.620] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003305)  
next to Negro-Soxidental.

**[02:55:56.400] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003306)  
And malaking epekto po ito, chairman, sa ating mga magsasaka.

**[02:56:02.260] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003307)  
Pag natamaan po silang ginagawa po nila,

**[02:56:04.460] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003308)  
itong SRA na pesting ito, kinakain po nila yung dahon ng mga tubo ng tanim.

**[02:56:13.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003309)  
At dahil dyan, napakalaki ng gastos, of course, if you use pesticides for the prevention of this.

**[02:56:20.860] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003310)  
Kung sa Bisaya, padayon lang po, padayon sa inyong programa.

**[02:56:24.060] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003311)  
At tuloy nyo lang po.

**[02:56:26.400] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003312)  
We just want you to know that we don't want to kill the sugar industry as well.

**[02:56:30.500] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003313)  
We want to make sure that the sugar industry survives.

**[02:56:33.800] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003314)  
This is still a very important product for our people, lalo na sa mga farmers.

**[02:56:39.920] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003315)  
And this time, 90% po, dati ka po sinabi nyo po, sugar farmers, mga hasyendero.

**[02:56:46.600] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003316)  
Ngayon po, 90% po ay small farmers.

**[02:56:49.940] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003317)  
These are agrarian reform beneficiaries.

**[02:56:52.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003318)  
And I'd like to ask an opinion of the good gentleman,

**[02:56:56.400] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003319)  
from Cabangkalan, my hometown of Cabangkalan,

**[02:57:00.520] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003320)  
on his opinion.

**[02:57:02.340] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003321)  
And if he needs more help, maybe he can say it today, Mr. Chairman,

**[02:57:07.980] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003322)  
lalo na in light of this fact of the RSSI and the El Nino.

**[02:57:12.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003323)  
May we recognize also a few moments to our SRA chairman.

**[02:57:16.340] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003324)  
Maybe he may need more help that we can infuse to the budget of the DA

**[02:57:19.980] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003325)  
to help on this crisis, Your Honor, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:57:23.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003326)  
Go ahead, go ahead, Mr. Escona.

**[02:57:26.400] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003327)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:57:27.780] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003328)  
Thank you, Senator Subiri.

**[02:57:31.480] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003329)  
Yes po, we've been fighting RSSI for a couple of years in Luzon

**[02:57:36.580] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003330)  
and in Negros, two years in Bukidnon.

**[02:57:40.160] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003331)  
This is the first year.

**[02:57:41.600] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003332)  
Last year, we tried our best to quarantine Bukidnon

**[02:57:45.660] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003333)  
and protect it from RSSI coming from Negros.

**[02:57:49.540] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003334)  
However, it arrived in Davao and somehow made its way to Bukidnon.

**[02:57:55.480] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003335)  
Now,

**[02:57:56.400] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003336)  
yes, we do need more support, especially budget-wise.

**[02:58:01.360] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003337)  
We greatly appreciate the help of the DA by extending budget down to SRA to help.

**[02:58:10.740] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003338)  
Concurrently, we're using pesticides, which is very expensive.

**[02:58:14.800] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003339)  
However, the Secretary already came up with a program wherein LGUs,

**[02:58:21.220] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003340)  
farmer associations, our meal district development councils,

**[02:58:25.380] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003341)  
which are private,

**[02:58:26.400] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003342)  
and other organizations are encouraged to make their own biocontrol laboratories.

**[02:58:34.680] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003343)  
SRA and the DA will provide the fungi, the mother fungi,

**[02:58:40.220] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003344)  
that we have tested, and it's proven to help control RSSI.

**[02:58:45.800] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003345)  
It's a lot slower than pesticide,

**[02:58:47.580] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003346)  
but we need to bring it down to the grassroots level,

**[02:58:51.600] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003347)  
Senator and Mr. Chair,

**[02:58:53.120] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003348)  
to make it accessible.

**[02:58:56.400] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003349)  
So, I think with your help, with the Chairman's help,

**[02:59:03.500] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003350)  
we do need budget for it.

**[02:59:06.260] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003351)  
There were already LGUs in Negros who signified that they are willing to go

**[02:59:13.500] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003352)  
and collaborate with the DA and organize these laboratories.

**[02:59:16.600] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003353)  
This is for the future use.

**[02:59:18.540] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003354)  
We believe RSSI will be here to stay.

**[02:59:21.060] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003355)  
It's very hard to totally eradicate.

**[02:59:22.900] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003356)  
But we just, according to the countries who

**[02:59:26.400] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003357)  
had RSSI or still have RSSI,

**[02:59:28.900] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003358)  
we just have to learn and live with it and control it in the biocontrol way.

**[02:59:32.520] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003359)  
We cannot continually use chemical pesticides.

**[02:59:35.740] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003360)  
It is not sustainable.

**[02:59:37.240] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003361)  
So, hopefully, hopefully,

**[02:59:39.620] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003362)  
the committee can give us an additional budget specifically for our SSI.

**[02:59:45.340] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003363)  
And on the other hand,

**[02:59:47.580] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003364)  
El Nino mitigation as well, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:59:50.400] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003365)  
Mr. Chair?

**[02:59:51.020] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003366)  
Mr. Chair.

**[02:59:53.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003367)  
Sorry.

**[02:59:54.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003368)  
Just on that, just I'd like to support,

**[02:59:56.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003369)  
Senator Subiri on that.

**[02:59:58.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003370)  
And Mr. Chairman, a bit of...

**[02:59:59.880] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003371)  
institutional memory. If you will

**[03:00:02.180] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003372)  
recall, the law that

**[03:00:04.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003373)  
created the sugar

**[03:00:06.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003374)  
the SIDA law actually

**[03:00:10.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003375)  
originally appropriated a

**[03:00:12.240] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003376)  
total amount of 2 billion

**[03:00:13.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003377)  
and that 2 billion was

**[03:00:16.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003378)  
reduced to 500 million

**[03:00:18.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003379)  
because of its incapacity to disperse

**[03:00:20.480] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003380)  
and spend except

**[03:00:21.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003381)  
on farm-to-market roads

**[03:00:24.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003382)  
and as a result,

**[03:00:26.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003383)  
they've had this very small budget

**[03:00:28.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003384)  
since then, but the original intent

**[03:00:30.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003385)  
which was long, long ago, 2 billion

**[03:00:32.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003386)  
na yan, na-reduce yan.

**[03:00:34.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003387)  
Kaya I think there is really room

**[03:00:35.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003388)  
given El Nino as well as RSSI

**[03:00:38.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003389)  
to increase it

**[03:00:40.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003390)  
as our Senator Subiri

**[03:00:42.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-003391)  
said.

**[03:00:43.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003392)  
And just to add, you have not

**[03:00:45.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003393)  
unlike the other agencies,

**[03:00:48.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003394)  
you have to submit your

**[03:00:50.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003395)  
request for additional funding. You have not

**[03:00:51.880] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003396)  
submitted it.

**[03:00:53.660] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003397)  
Okay. Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:00:55.680] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003398)  
We'll do it.

**[03:00:56.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003399)  
We intend

**[03:00:57.980] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003400)  
to finalize our

**[03:00:59.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003401)  
subcommittee report by Friday, so

**[03:01:01.980] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003402)  
you have to submit it before then.

**[03:01:03.980] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-003403)  
Okay, Mr. Chair. Thank you very much.

**[03:01:05.940] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003404)  
On a final note, Mr. Chair, because I have a meeting

**[03:01:08.100] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003405)  
with the JAICA head, I think they're worried

**[03:01:10.120] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003406)  
about their, not at 1 o'clock,

**[03:01:12.320] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003407)  
they were worried about the

anong

**[03:16:26.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003836)

**[03:16:29.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003837)

**[03:16:34.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003838)

**[03:17:01.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003841)

**[03:17:06.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003842)

**[03:17:19.080] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003844)

**[03:17:26.920] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003845)

**[03:17:29.600] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003846)

**[03:17:35.320] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003847)  
sa pag-imbita sa aming civil society group sa budget hearing.

**[03:17:39.020] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003848)  
This is the first time in at least 10 years that civil society has been invited as resource persons in budget hearing.

**[03:17:45.560] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003849)  
So thank you, Sen. Kiko, for maintaining the momentum of budget reforms started by SP Wynn Gatchalian and Finance Committee Chair JV Ejercito,

**[03:17:53.540] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003850)

**[03:17:56.360] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003851)

**[03:18:02.040] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003852)

**[03:18:08.860] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003853)

**[03:18:15.300] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003854)

**[03:18:24.800] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003855)

**[03:28:08.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003959)  
That's not in the transcript kasi so I had to put it on record.

**[03:28:13.240] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003960)  
We are very happy with the thumbs up, Your Honor.

**[03:28:17.060] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003961)  
Third and finally po,

**[03:28:18.400] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003962)  
we hope po we can involve the scientists in the review of the budget

**[03:28:22.300] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003963)  
to address El Niño, to double-check many of these coordinates that we think have errors in them,

**[03:28:28.000] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003964)  
and promote the stability of the agriculture prices.

**[03:28:30.880] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003965)  
At sa tingin po namin, it's part of the institutionalization of the Budget Monitoring Group,

**[03:28:36.140] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003966)  
Budget Monitoring Network, is to be able to fund these third-party research institutions

**[03:28:42.440] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003967)  
so that they can really attend and do detailed analyses and studies of these projects.

**[03:28:48.240] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003968)  
Muli po, nagpapasalamat po kami kasi ito po ay napakahalagang

**[03:28:52.140] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003970)  
oportunidad para po sa aming mga budget watchdogs na mapakinggan.

**[03:28:57.120] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003971)  
At si Tim po ay mayroon din po siyang mga inputs galing po sa mga farmers and fisher folks,

**[03:29:03.100] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-003972)  
fisher folk groups, and may we ask po that Tim be recognized, Mr. Chairman.

**[03:29:07.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003973)  
Go ahead, Tim.

**[03:29:08.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003974)  
Mr. Chairman.

**[03:29:09.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003975)  
Yes.

**[03:29:09.800] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003976)  
Just on the topic ni Ken, kasi nabuksan niya yung farm-to-market roads,

**[03:29:16.660] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003977)  
and I just want to share with the Secretary,

**[03:29:20.540] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003978)  
alam ko linilinis na ito ng department, pero mayroon pa rin kaming nakitang mga,

**[03:29:25.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003979)  
we call it red flags rin, in line with what Ken mentioned.

**[03:29:30.640] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003980)  
And Mr. Chairman, thank you rin for inviting the People's Budget Coalition.

**[03:29:37.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003981)  
We've been working with them since last year.

**[03:29:39.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003982)  
In fact, malaki po yung kanilang papel kung paano natin inayos yung 2026 budget.

**[03:29:47.660] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003983)  
At dahil sa pagtutulungan natin,

**[03:29:52.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003984)  
yung recent Supreme Court ruling, hindi apektado yung 2026 budget.

**[03:29:57.420] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003985)  
It's only on the 2024 and 2025 budget.

**[03:30:00.280] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003988)  
And that's also with the close coordination with our civil society organization, kay Sen. Kiko, nung hinimay niya mga farm-to-market roads at irrigation projects.

**[03:30:13.280] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003989)  
So, Secretary, may nakita pa kami yung tawag namin mga red flags, mga 29 projects worth P660 million.

**[03:30:21.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003990)  
I know this is not big compared to the P16 billion total FMR.

**[03:30:28.180] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003991)  
Pero dahil yung Kongreso nagpasok ng P6 billion, hindi namin alam kung yung P6 billion na yun ay naayon sa inyong master list or master plan.

**[03:30:42.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003992)  
So, but nakita namin may mga ganitong mga red flag.

**[03:30:47.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003993)  
Next slide, Elena.

**[03:30:48.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003994)  
First yung the same name.

**[03:30:51.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003995)  
So, nakita namin doon sa mga FMR may mga pare-pareho yung pangalan.

**[03:30:56.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003996)  
Next.

**[03:30:58.400] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003997)  
The same name, but different project coordinates.

**[03:31:03.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003998)  
Pag tinignan natin yung pangalan, pare-pareho, pero hindi man pareho yung coordinates.

**[03:31:07.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003999)  
And then next.

**[03:31:10.340] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004000)  
Different name, but the same coordinates.

**[03:31:13.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004001)  
Ito mas delikado ito dahil iba yung pangalan, pero parehong coordinates.

**[03:31:18.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004002)  
Yun yung sinabi kanina ni Ken.

**[03:31:20.260] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004003)  
Baka.

**[03:31:21.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004004)  
Nasa ibang lugar.

**[03:31:22.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004005)  
I think, baka may, to give the benefit of the doubt, baka mali lang i-input, no, nung numbers.

**[03:31:28.080] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004006)  
Kasi puro numbers yan.

**[03:31:29.720] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004007)  
But when I received a briefing from the People's Budget Coalition, may mga coordinates na nasa, no, nasa Benham Rise.

**[03:31:39.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004008)  
Yung iba, nasa ibang, hindi nasa Pilipinas, no.

**[03:31:43.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004009)  
And then next, ito rin, delikado rin ito.

**[03:31:47.800] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004010)  
So, the same project, the same coordinates, reappearing independently.

**[03:31:51.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004011)  
Different years.

**[03:31:52.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004012)  
No?

**[03:31:52.780] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004013)  
And we only compared 2026 and 2027.

**[03:31:57.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004014)  
Baka meron pa yan sa 2025, 2024.

**[03:32:01.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004015)  
So, fina-flag lang namin in department.

**[03:32:03.720] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004016)  
We just want to let the department know may mga ganitong red flags.

**[03:32:07.840] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004017)  
We'll be more than happy to share with the department itong list para malinis ninyo yung inlistahan.

**[03:32:12.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004018)  
And with the recent amendment or insertion ng house na 6 billion, we will also review this.

**[03:32:21.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004019)  
In cooperation with our civil society organization.

**[03:32:25.200] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004020)  
But sa department, gawin nyo na rin yung review.

**[03:32:30.200] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004021)  
At yun naman yung agreement natin, dapat pasok siya doon sa inyong master plan.

**[03:32:35.560] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004022)  
Para everything is coordinated with the department.

**[03:32:39.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004023)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[03:32:41.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-004024)  
Thank you, Senate President, Mr. President.

**[03:32:44.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-004025)  
Go ahead, Tim. You have the floor.

**[03:32:47.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004026)  
Magandang hapon po.

**[03:32:48.380] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004027)  
Timothy Salamon from People's Budget Coalition.

**[03:32:50.500] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004028)  
Representing Task Force Mapalad National Peasant Federation.

**[03:32:54.040] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004029)  
One million farmers across the nation that availed of the land rights through the Agrarian Reform Program.

**[03:33:00.260] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004030)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[03:33:01.620] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004031)  
Actually, pinapunta ko dito ng elders ko, si na Christian Munzod, Atty. Christian Munzod.

**[03:33:06.600] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004032)  
Atty. Manding Harilia.

**[03:33:08.060] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004033)  
They wanted me to take an angry tone.

**[03:33:11.200] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004034)  
Pero upon hearing the discussion earlier, sabi ko kay Atty. Christian.

**[03:33:15.820] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004035)  
Atty. Christian, parang binibigay nila yung gusto nila atin makuha.

**[03:33:19.400] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004036)  
Natuwa po kami.

**[03:33:20.500] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004037)  
Yung doon sa discussion regarding doon sa RSSI.

**[03:33:24.260] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004038)  
We do note just some further details on this.

**[03:33:28.140] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004039)  
Napakalaking bagay po kasi nito.

**[03:33:34.660] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004040)  
Ano po eh, nabanggit kanina, napakamahal nung insecticides that are being purchased for RSSI.

**[03:33:43.140] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004041)  
I believe from 10,000 per hectare po iyon.

**[03:33:46.040] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004042)  
And we do note na napakababa ng budget na binigay sa SRE.

**[03:33:50.500] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004043)  
To deal with that insect.

**[03:33:52.940] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004044)  
And natutuwa po kami na the SRE has already mentioned that there are biocontrol mechanisms to deal with this.

**[03:34:00.440] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004045)  
We already submit this to the DA and we are glad na nakinig po kayo sa recommendations sa civil society.

**[03:34:06.860] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004046)  
In fact, 10,000 per hectare po yung synthetic pesticide.

**[03:34:12.520] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004047)  
Yung sinasuggest po namin na biocontrol.

**[03:34:14.660] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004048)  
2,600 per hectare lang.

**[03:34:16.820] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004049)  
Nabibili po mo siya Shopee.

**[03:34:18.620] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004050)  
Tapos pag binili mo siya Shopee,

**[03:34:20.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004051)  
pwede mo siyang padamihin sa isang lab na kayang gawin ng mga agricultural students.

**[03:34:26.500] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004052)  
So, we do support that budget.

**[03:34:28.640] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004053)  
Sana pataasin po yung pagkontrol mismo dito sa peste na ito.

**[03:34:31.940] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004054)  
In fact, itong peste na RSSI, kapatid po siya ng cocolisap.

**[03:34:36.100] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004055)  
Sen Pangilinan.

**[03:34:37.540] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004056)  
Pareho silang, they bore into the actual cane.

**[03:34:42.520] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004057)  
Tapos sisip-sipin ngayon tamis.

**[03:34:43.920] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004058)  
Kaya siya masama siya sa sugar.

**[03:34:46.280] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004059)  
So, I also wanted to,

**[03:34:47.980] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004060)  
kung ano po yung ginawa,

**[03:34:50.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004061)  
kung ano po yung ginawa nyo sa coconut,

**[03:34:52.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004062)  
please do it with sugar.

**[03:34:54.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004063)  
Kasi ang dami pong nawawalan ng hanap buhay dahil dito siya sa cocolisap.

**[03:34:59.540] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004064)  
I just wanted to follow up po.

**[03:35:02.540] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004065)  
Deliver niyo po yung budget na yung DA.

**[03:35:04.540] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004066)  
Hingin niyo po sa Senate yung susuportahan po ng civil society.

**[03:35:07.540] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004067)  
Yung hihingin ninyo.

**[03:35:09.540] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004068)  
Kasi napakadami po talaga ng apektado.

**[03:35:11.540] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004069)  
Pangalawa po,

**[03:35:13.540] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004070)  
hindi lang po sana namin gusto gawin ito sa sugar.

**[03:35:17.540] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004071)  
Nabanggit na rin doon sa usapan with

**[03:35:20.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004072)  
Sen Zubiri

**[03:35:22.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004073)  
na napakalaki nung issue nung access to fertilizer and pesticides na imported.

**[03:35:26.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004074)  
And we do note that

**[03:35:28.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004075)  
bukod sa mahal,

**[03:35:30.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004076)  
nakakasira siya sa lupa.

**[03:35:32.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004077)  
Tapos there are actually reports that

**[03:35:34.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004078)  
we are still using about seven fertilizers,

**[03:35:37.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004079)  
particularly ang banana,

**[03:35:39.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004080)  
na banned na sa European Union and the US.

**[03:35:43.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004081)  
We need to take it.

**[03:35:45.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004082)  
We will submit you the list of the actual names of the fertilizers.

**[03:35:49.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004083)  
So this is the point is to say

**[03:35:51.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004084)  
that we wanted to veer away from importing fertilizers and pesticides.

**[03:35:55.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004085)  
And to develop our own industry.

**[03:35:57.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004086)  
To develop more nature-based and farmer-friendly inputs to farming.

**[03:36:04.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004087)  
In essence kasi,

**[03:36:06.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004088)  
you take away the money from importing.

**[03:36:08.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004089)  
Pag ininvest mo siya sa baba,

**[03:36:10.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004090)  
magdadagdag ka ng value na pwede i-create nung farmer.

**[03:36:13.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004091)  
Dagdag income sa kanya kasi yun e

**[03:36:15.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004092)  
pag siya yung gumawa ng mismo fertilizer.

**[03:36:17.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004093)  
Pag mayroon mo pa yung lupa kasi hindi mo sinira because of synthetic fertilizer.

**[03:36:21.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004094)  
So we do encourage this.

**[03:36:23.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004095)  
Sana po makita namin ito.

**[03:36:24.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004096)  
Ang tagal na namin pinapush ito throughout all of the commodity chains.

**[03:36:30.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004097)  
So yun po yung mga natutuwa kami.

**[03:36:32.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004098)  
Some points that we remain concerned about.

**[03:36:36.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004099)  
Mayroon po rin pong reports that

**[03:36:38.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004100)  
I think Inquire published that about 40% of our farming and fishing population

**[03:36:44.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004101)  
remains to be registered with the RSVSA.

**[03:36:47.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004102)  
So we wanted to press na

**[03:36:49.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004103)  
sana may action plan to deal with this gap on registration.

**[03:36:53.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004104)  
From what we also understand,

**[03:36:55.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004105)  
most of the consequential services of the DA require

**[03:36:58.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004106)  
some form of registration.

**[03:37:01.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004107)  
Magiging farmers association or fisher folk association.

**[03:37:05.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004108)  
And yet the numbers do tell,

**[03:37:07.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004109)  
and this is from a report by the ATI themselves,

**[03:37:10.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004110)  
na 80% lang ng farmers natin ang may access to organizational registration.

**[03:37:15.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004111)  
So in essence,

**[03:37:16.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004112)  
by that policy,

**[03:37:17.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004113)  
only 20% are reached by very big agricultural support services.

**[03:37:24.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004114)  
And ito po, problema ko po yun kasi ang laki po ng budget ng DA.

**[03:37:30.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004115)  
Ang ibig sabihin mo ba nun 20% lang talaga yung balak natin pagsisilbihan?

**[03:37:34.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004116)  
We already suggested from the last budget,

**[03:37:38.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004117)  
nag-participate din po kasi kami doon.

**[03:37:40.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004118)  
We issued questions to the House of Representatives sa DA last year.

**[03:37:44.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004119)  
Nag-commit po ang ating DA to invest on community organizing

**[03:37:48.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004120)  
or in improving yung pag-fe-federate ng mga farmers

**[03:37:53.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004121)  
into actual agricultural cooperatives.

**[03:37:55.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004122)  
Hindi ko po nakita where that promise went.

**[03:37:58.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004123)  
Pero I do recognize that this is already in the bill of the good senator na ano yung

**[03:38:04.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004124)  
agricultural extension and cooperatives bills.

**[03:38:07.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004125)  
Bukod po dito, hindi naman po kailangan po yung batas,

**[03:38:11.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004126)  
but we also note na hindi din po gumagalaw yung,

**[03:38:14.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004127)  
civil society led na Philippine Action Plan for Family Farming.

**[03:38:18.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004128)  
We already participated heavily on this.

**[03:38:20.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004129)  
It was inked on 2019.

**[03:38:22.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004130)  
Ang pinaka thesis po, pinaka point po ng policy na ito,

**[03:38:27.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004131)  
is to ensure that agricultural services do not remain with yung SUKI system,

**[03:38:32.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004132)  
yung mga laging binibigyan,

**[03:38:34.160] CODE-NGO Project Coordinator Timothy Salomon** (asr-004133)

**[05:29:55.940] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006005)

**[05:29:58.080] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006006)

**[05:30:00.000] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006007)  
Noon po kasing 2022 to 2024, it was the DPWH who was implementing the erection ng warehouse.

**[05:30:13.700] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006008)

**[05:30:20.380] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006009)  
At ang nangyari doon sa 29 facilities, ang natapos po roon ay 16.

**[05:30:26.520] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006010)  
11 yung 16 yung natapos, 11 yung hindi natapos.

**[05:30:32.420] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006011)  
Pero sa 16 facilities po walang mga building permits.

**[05:30:37.240] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006012)

**[05:30:40.740] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006013)  
Ngayon po, ngayong 2025 onwards, inilipad na po sa Philmec yung construction ng building.

**[05:30:49.520] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006014)

**[05:30:53.240] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006015)  
So yun po yung update.

**[05:30:55.380] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006016)

**[05:30:56.520] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006017)

**[05:31:01.160] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006018)

**[05:31:03.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006019)

**[05:31:07.620] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006020)

**[05:31:20.080] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006026)

**[05:31:20.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006027)

**[05:31:23.640] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006028)

**[05:31:28.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-006030)

**[05:31:35.100] PhilMech Director IV Dionisio G. Alvindia, Ph.D.** (asr-006033)

**[05:32:15.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006043)  
Just a few more questions.

**[05:32:17.480] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006044)  
Dumating si Senator Aimee na alala ko tuloy.

**[05:32:22.740] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006045)  
Doon sa issue nung seized Vietnam rights,

**[05:32:26.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006046)  
we would like to clarify ano na ang status nitong dalawa yan actually, yung Limay-Bataan and Davao.

**[05:32:40.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006047)  
And my understanding is the delivery dates based on the documentation were delayed both for Limay-Bataan and Davao.

**[05:32:56.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006048)  
The SP6 specified a specific date that it should arrive.

**[05:33:01.740] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006049)  
Pero lumampas na, both for Bataan and for Davao.

**[05:33:07.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006050)  
And that's why, as I understand it, it was in violation of the permits.

**[05:33:18.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006051)  
And therefore, kaya finlag at eventually, sumubok tumakas.

**[05:33:24.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006052)

**[05:33:29.740] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006053)  
At na-kumpis ka, na-intercept.

**[05:33:34.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006054)  
So that's question number one.

**[05:33:36.760] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006055)

**[05:33:50.740] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006056)  
Sino sa PPI ang lumabag?

**[05:33:54.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006057)

**[05:33:57.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006058)  
Kasi iba yung sabi nga ninyo, na-delay.

**[05:34:01.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006059)  
Dapat yan July, pero September na.

**[05:34:04.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006060)  
So, are you, is there an internal investigation?

**[05:34:11.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006061)

**[05:34:20.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006062)

**[05:34:33.200] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006063)

**[05:34:35.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006064)  
Ito ang tinatanong din.

**[05:34:40.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006065)  
And I'm sure tatanongin ito sa plenary.

**[05:34:43.980] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006066)  
Go ahead, sir.

**[05:34:45.020] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006067)  
Mr. Chair.

**[05:34:48.160] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006068)

**[05:34:50.180] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006069)

**[05:34:58.320] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006070)  
wala silang nakikitang issue sa documents.

**[05:35:02.380] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006071)

**[05:35:11.300] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006072)  
Meron din sinasabi na yung must-depart date, hindi na meet.

**[05:35:16.580] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006073)  
But then the bill of lading issued by the shipping company,

**[05:35:20.180] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006074)  
was August 31.

**[05:35:23.020] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006075)

**[05:35:28.760] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006076)

**[05:35:30.380] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006077)  
So, it's a BOC matter now.

**[05:35:33.480] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006078)

**[05:35:41.760] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006079)

**[05:35:47.180] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006080)

**[05:35:50.180] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006081)  
And the agriculture, except for issuance of an SPSIC.

**[05:35:55.560] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006082)

**[05:36:01.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006083)  
Before lang, sorry, just before Davao.

**[05:36:04.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006084)

**[05:36:10.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006085)

**[05:36:13.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006086)

**[05:36:14.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006087)  
Pero, hindi nga transshipment.

**[05:36:17.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006088)

**[05:36:20.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006089)  
So, may mga ganong problema nun.

**[05:36:24.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006090)

**[05:36:26.760] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006091)

**[05:36:28.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-006092)
